公司簡介 
鴻海為專業 ODM/EMS 廠商，產品涵蓋手
機、伺服器、平板、網通設備等終端產品，
以及各類電子零組件如連接器、連接線等，
旗下重要子公司包含工業富聯 (601138 
SH)、富智康 (2038 HK) 及鴻騰精密 (6088 
HK)，近年公司開始積極投入研發資源在電
動車、數位醫療、機器人、AI、半導體、5G
等領域。1Q26 產品營收占比：消費暨智能
33%、雲端網路 48%、電腦終端 14%、元
件及其他 5%。

主要客戶 ：Apple、HP、Dell、Sony、Cisco、
北美四大 CSP 等 
主要競爭對手：

黃璽銘 hsiming.huang@sinopac.com 
本次報告：電話訪談

鴻海 (2317 TT) 
同時受惠 GPU、ASIC AI 伺服器建置

永豐觀點 
關注 CSP 支出 AI 伺服器的產品組合需求變化。 
 投資評價與建議 
研究部維持鴻海買進建議：新增預期 2027 年鴻海增添 ASIC AI 伺服器機櫃出貨，
雖然短期 VR200 NVL72 出貨受限，鴻海仍會維持 VR200 主要供應商地位，持續
受惠 AI 伺服器發展趨勢。除機櫃組裝以外，增加供應零組件產值。整體營收規模
受惠 AI 伺服器擴張的同時平衡獲利率、獲利金額以及營運資金運用。預估鴻海
2026 年/2027 年稅後 EPS 17.92 元/23.36 元。後續關注 CSP 支出 AI 伺服器的產
品 組 合 需 求 變 化 。 維 持 鴻 海 買 進 建 議 ， 因 獲 利 預 估 上 修目 標 價 至350 元
(15x2027FEPS)。

ESG 評析 
鴻海企業永續評鑑整體為 A 等，於 SASB 產業永續議題與跨產業五大永續面向平
均得分分別為 A 等與 A 等。 
SinoPac+ ESG 評鑑系統評等

資料來源：SinoPac+ ESG 評等 (依循 2025 SinoPac+企業永續評鑑方法學)

專業意見，對於評分結果及評估報告內容之完整性及真實性，不負擔保責任，亦不對閱讀或使用
本評估報告之第三方負任何責任。 
註 2：永豐投顧已獲 SASB 授權，於研究中使用 SASB 重大性地圖之一般議題分類及揭露主題。

### Page 2

2

鴻海為專業 ODM/EMS 廠商，產品涵蓋手機、伺服器、平板、網通設備等終端產
品，以及各類電子零組件如連接器、連接線等，旗下重要子公司包含工業富聯
(601138 SH)、富智康(2038 HK)及鴻騰精密(6088 HK)，近年公司開始積極投入研
發資源在電動車 、 數位醫療 、 機器人 、AI、 半導體 、5G 等領域 。 主要客戶為Apple、
HP、Dell、Sony、Cisco、北美四大 CSP 等。1Q26 產品營收占比：消費暨智能
33%、雲端網路 48%、電腦終端 14%、元件及其他 5%。 
2Q26 營收優於預期：鴻海 2Q26 營收 2.51 兆，達成率 107%優於預期，受惠 AI
伺服器強勁成長、筆電漲價及舊世代手機拉貨。3Q26 AI 伺服器 GB300 機櫃出貨
持續季增，手機則逐漸進入新品拉貨期。研究部維持預期 VR200 機櫃組裝出貨落
於 4Q26，但上游供應鏈遞延影響整體出貨量，原估4Q26 整體出貨 5,000 櫃，調
整至 2,500 櫃，惟整機櫃單價因關鍵零組件漲價，增加約 15%~20%，以及增加
GB300 機櫃出貨，抵銷出貨量下滑影響。因2Q26 營收基期墊高，上修預估3Q26
營收至 2.69 兆元。4Q26 在手機新品及 GB 持續出貨營收季增 13%。 
關注 CSP 支出 AI 伺服器的產品組合：AI 伺服器需求近年以美系 CSP、AI 模型商
及 Neocloud 驅動，中期則有政府建置主權 AI、企業導入 AI 應用增添需求。整體
市場以 CoWoS 產能推估輝達 2027 年 VR200 NVL72 機櫃出貨量，目前研究部維
持中性預估約 40,000~50,000 櫃(市場預期多為 60,000~70,000 櫃)，認為其他晶
片為以 HGX 架構形式或 GB300 機櫃出貨。主要考量 Rubin 晶片 3Q26 遞延、
VR200 NVL72 機櫃建置成本上揚及 CSP 的即時算力需求，支出將優先建置其他
架構規格或自家 ASIC AI 伺服器。待 VR200 NVL72 機櫃順利量產後，視 CSP 需
求上修機櫃出貨比重。 
增添 ASIC AI 伺服器機櫃業務： 雖然研究部維持較市場中性預估 2027 年整體
VR200 NVL72 機櫃出貨量，但預期鴻海維持 VR200 的主要供應商角色，整體市
占率達 40%以上，部分供應客戶超過 50%比重。另外，研究部新增預期鴻海切入
美系 CSP AI ASIC 伺服器機櫃供應，推算有助於增加獲利貢獻約8%。鴻海在 GPU 
AI 伺服器主要適用 Buy and Sell 模式，ASIC AI 伺服器適用 Consignment 模式，
有助於營收規模擴張的同時平衡獲利率、 獲利金額以及營運資金運用。 鴻海不只機
櫃出貨規模擴張，同時增加 AI 伺服器機櫃中零組件供應比重，如 Midplane、水
冷零組件、交換機等，增添 AI 伺服器機櫃產值貢獻。 
綜合以上，調整後預估 2026 年營收 10.4 兆，年增 28%，毛利率 6.1%，營益率
3.5%，稅後淨利 2,505 億，年增 32%，稅後 EPS 17.92 元。2027 年，預估營收
14.2 兆元，年增 37%，毛利率 5.5%，營益率 3.2%，稅後淨利 3,261 億元，年增
30%，稅後 EPS 23.36 元。

華邦電 (2344 TT)

靜待投資人重拾 AI 硬體信心

維持買進評等，目標價上調至 208 元，潛在上漲空間 34% 
1. 2Q26 毛利率估逾 6 成，稅後挑戰逾 300 億元。 
2. 路竹廠二期提前規畫，CUBE 與矽電容產品具潛力。 
3. FY27-28 毛利率低點估 58.7%、高點估逾 7 成。

2Q26 毛利率估逾 6 成，稅後挑戰逾 300 億元 
華邦電 2Q26 合併營收 598.43 億元，QoQ+56.4%，YoY+184.7%，數字優於富邦
預期。其中，新唐營收占 13.3%，QoQ-0.6%；不含新唐之營收占 86.7%，
QoQ+70.9%。其次，富邦推測本季 DRAM、SLC 及 NOR 各自漲幅約達 5、6 成；
加上公司已公告 4 月自結數，因此推測 2Q26 毛利率約達 66.9%。簡言之，我們預
估華邦電 2Q26 營業利益約達 303.23 億元，稅前淨利估 304.14 億元，歸屬⺟公司
稅後淨利估 243.95 億元，EPS 估 5.42 元。(以目前普通股本 450.00 億元計算，未
考慮海外可轉債餘額 7.50 億美元之稀釋效果；目前轉換價 108.94 元)

NOR 價格：台廠下半年漲勢穩健 
兆易創新 6 月 30 日公告指出 ， 當前記憶體產品價格上漲主要來自於 AI 需求顯著增
加使得國際大廠將經營重心切換至相關產品領域；而利基存儲市場係間接受益於供
應緊張造成的外溢效果。該公告直指下游需求因記憶體大漲已使需求受到⼀定程度
的抑制，在未來利基型存儲市場產能的邊際增加，價格將出現相當幅度的回落。綜
合各方資訊，我們推測 NOR Flash 價格出現分岐變化；陸廠價格漲幅加速收斂，而
台廠下半年價格仍有不小漲幅。推測原因為台廠以伺服器、網通及車用市場為主；
而陸廠則以消費性市場為主，因此才出現 NOR 價格走勢分化的現象。

3Q26 售價季增估逾 3 成，EPS 預估升抵 9.45 元 
華邦電所處利基型 DRAM 與 SLC 市場 3Q26 呈現嚴重供不應求，預料價格季漲幅
達 3 成或以上；NOR Flash 方面受惠 CSP 大客戶備貨積極，排擠其他應用的產能
供應，因此整體價格也看到類似 DRAM 的漲幅水準。因此，我們預估華邦電 3Q26
合併營收達 814.66 億元，QoQ+36.1%，YoY+274.2%；價格漲勢續推升毛利率上
揚至 76.0%。簡言之，我們預估華邦電 3Q26 營業利益達 507.57 億元，歸屬⺟公
司稅後淨利估 425.09 億元，EPS 估 9.45 元。

富邦不同之處 
富邦目前對華邦電 2026-27 年 EPS 預估值分別為 27.74 、 51.83 元 ， 相較 CMoney
同業預估值分別為+30%、+43%，明顯較為樂觀。

評價與風險 
儘管記憶體產業目前正處投資人擔心高檔反轉之際，然而產業在 SCA/LTA 的商業
型態支撐下，我們看好此次產業周期底部將遠高於歷史區間，因此維持華邦電買進
的投資評等，目標價設定為 208.0 元，約為 FY26 之 7.5XPER，華邦電的投資風險
主要來自：1)DDR4、DDR3 價格走勢不如預期，2)擴產進度或生產良率低於預期。

路竹廠二期提前規畫 CUBE 與矽電容具潛力 
代理式 AI 帶動記憶體與先進封裝需求的成⻑預期將持續⼀段相當⻑的時間；因此
華邦電為因應⻑期需求成⻑正計畫啟動產能擴充計畫，將路竹廠原定 2、3 期合併
開發，最快 2027 年施工興建無塵室、下單採購設備，最快 2029 年可完工量產。
除利基型 DRAM 以外，華邦電的 CUBE 客製化記憶體(for Edge AI/Physical AI)與
矽電容(for ASIC+HBM 先進封裝)製程能力皆佔據潛在市場的有利位置，故新建廠
房有助公司及時取得市場先機，保有快速擴產的彈性。

毛利率高點估逾 7 成，FY28 低點估 58.7%  
華邦電 4Q26 價格漲勢預料縮窄至 2~5%，銷售量受惠產能擴充而有所成⻑，推測
合併營收升抵 888 億元， QoQ+9.0% ； 毛利率估微升至 76.9% ， 單季 EPS 估約 10.62
元，累積全年 EPS 約 27.74 元。展望後市，我們預測記憶體價格 2H27 開始出現有
意義的修正，並持續到 1H28，累積波段跌幅估約 35-41%；記憶體毛利率低點預
估落在 60.5%。在上述框架下，富邦預估華邦電 2027、28 年 EPS 分別為 51.83、
37.17 元，合併毛利率高點約為 76.9%、低點約為 58.7%。

富邦的股票評等標準 
評等  定義 
買進 預估未來6個月內的絕對報酬超過15%  
中立  預估未來6個月內有絕對報酬介於15%與負15%之間 
賣出 預估未來6個月內的絕對報酬高於負15% 
未評等 由於富邦目前與該公司有特定交易或沒有足夠的基本資料判斷該公司評等 
評估中 目前正在研議個股的投資評等，將於3到6個月內提供投資評等

產業評等 定義 
優於大盤 預估該產業在未來6個月內會比大盤指數表現突出 
持平 預估該產業在未來6個月內與大盤指數表現相較持平 
劣於大盤 預估該產業在未來6個月內會比大盤指數表現較差

This report has been produced in Chinese. In the event of questions regarding the accuracy of the translation, please refer to the original Chinese version.

Fubon's Rating System 
Rating Definition 
Buy Expected absolute return to be over 15% within the next 6 months 
Neutral  Expected absolute return to be level within the next 6 months 
Sell  Expected absolute return to be over negative 15% within the next 6 months 
Not Rated (NR)  Pursuant to Fubon acting in deals involving the company or there is not a sufficient fundamental basis for dete rmining an investment rating 
Under Review Fubon is in the process of determining an investment rating and will assign a rating within the 3- to 6-month hori zon

Sector Rating Definition 
Overweight Sector expected to outperform the broader market indexes within the next 6-12 months 
Market Weight  Sector expected to perform in line with the broader market averages within the next 6-12 months

個股報告

順德(2351) 
Q2 優預期，H2展望佳 
投資評等 目標價 
逢低買進 $240 
前次投資建議：2026.05.08 
「買進」 ，目標價: $240

報告日期：2026.07.16 研 究 員：邱岳霖

摘 要 及 調 整 原 因 
➢ 車用持續復與 AI導線架需求強勁，26Q2 營收遠優預期，EPS 1.7元 
➢ AI 導線架動能強勁，車市需求明顯回溫，均熱片新案持續發酵，2026 年 EPS 6.5 元，將創
歷史新高，兆豐投顧維持目標價 240 元(37X 26(F)PER)，潛在上漲空間 19.7%，投資評等為
「逢低買進」 。

基本資料 結論與建議 
2026/7/15 收盤價： 200.50 ➢ 順德 (2351)公告 6月營收：營收 12.1億元， MoM 
+5.0%/YoY +45.1% ， 26Q2營收 35.5億元 ，QoQ 
+26.4%/YoY +36.6%，優於預期，主要受惠車用與消
費性需求同步回溫、AI 導線架需求高速增長；同時在
報價調漲與稼動率增加下，預估毛利率18.5%，維持高
檔水準，反映營收優於預期，同步上修獲利預估至稅後
淨利3.1億元，QoQ +39.5%/YoY 虧為盈，EPS 1.7元
(原估1.35元)。 
➢ 展望2026年，順德最大成長動能來自 AI產品線。隨伺
服器電源架構升級，PSU瓦數提升帶動高功率導線架需
求全面激發，預期 2026年AI營收倍數增長，單月貢獻
有機會超過10%。車用方面，庫存調整結束、電動車需
求激增，全年可望呈雙位數成長。均熱片方面，今年3
月已出貨美系網通大廠，並切入美系記憶體大廠，受惠
記憶體市況火熱，營收規模有望擴大；更大尺寸
CPU/GPU用產品陸續驗證出貨，成長動能可延續至
2028年。毛利率方面，受惠26H1漲價效益、稼動率維
持高檔及產品組合轉佳，本業獲利能力可望持續改善。 
➢ 綜上，考量H1財報優於預期、展望轉趨正向，兆豐投
顧上修順德2026年營收至136.4億元，YoY +33.1%，
毛利率 18.8%（+5.5ppt） ，稅後淨利11.9億元， YoY 
+291.7%，EPS 6.5元（原估5.7元），兆豐投顧維持目
標價240元(37X 26(F)PER)，潛在上漲空間19.7%，投
資評等為「逢低買進」 。

- 2 - 
個股報告 
資料來源：CMoney；兆豐投顧預估

營 運 分 析

順德 2025年跨入 AI server領域，2026年散熱領域正式發酵 
順德(2351)成立於 1967 年，以辦公文具起家，1983 年跨入半導體導線架，
並於 2010 年與日本三菱伸銅(mitsubishi-shindoh)技術合作，取得導線架原
料銅合金製程設備及技術授權，目前產品涵蓋 IC 導線架、功率/分離式元件
導線架、LED 導線架、音圈馬達彈片，終端應用遍及車用、工業與消費性電
子。順德目前在全球導線架市佔排名前五，但在 Power IC 導線架則排名第
一。順德過往多以車用產品為主，不過隨著資料中心電源架構的改變，順德
與英飛凌將 800V 車用電源管理經驗移轉至資料中心，順德成為全台唯一 AI 
server 導線架主力供應商，並於 2025 年開始出高功率導線架與穩壓器。而
順德自 2024 年布局均熱片跨足散領域，並於 2026 年開始出 CPO Switch產
品，而多家 CSP之 CPU 與 GPU 也有望於本年度驗證後開始出貨。

圖一、順德功率導線架市佔率全球第一

- 3 - 
個股報告 
車用持續復甦與 AI導線架需求強勁，26Q2 營收遠優預期，EPS 1.7元 
順德(2351)公告 6月營收：營收12.1 億元，MoM +5.0%/YoY +45.1%，26Q2
營收 35.5 億元，QoQ +26.4%/YoY +36.6%，優於預期，主要受惠車用與消
費性需求同步回溫、AI 導線架需求高速增長；同時在報價調漲與稼動率增加
下，預估毛利率 18.5%，維持高檔水準，反映營收優於預期，同步上修獲利
預估至稅後淨利 3.1 億元，QoQ +39.5%/YoY 虧為盈，EPS 1.7元(原估 1.35
元)。

車用展望佳、AI 放量加速，上修獲利預估，26Q3 EPS 1.8 元 
展望 26Q3，兆豐投顧上修營收至 36.2 億元，QoQ +2.0%/YoY +42.2%，再
創新高，主要動能來自車用需求全面復甦與 AI 導線架供不應求，公司持續
透過設備擴充，均熱片也開始有所貢獻，預期營運動能將持續增長，在產品
組合優化與稼動率提升下，預估毛利率 18.5%，與上季相當，稅後淨利 3.2
億元，QoQ +3.2%/YoY +265.5%，EPS 1.8 元。

AI 導線架動能強勁，車市需求明顯回溫，均熱片新案持續發酵，2026年將
重返榮耀，上修 EPS至 6.5元 
展望 2026 年，順德最大成長動能來自AI產品線，隨著伺服器電源架構改變，
PSU瓦數持續提升，高功率導線架需求將被徹底激發，從高壓電網、SST(固
態變壓器)、Power Stage、48V bus converter、800V HVDC與 BBU皆須用
到高功率導線架，而順德自 2024 年攜手英飛凌（IFX）將 800V車用電源管
理經驗移轉至資料中心，使順德成為全球 AI server 導線架主力供應商之一
(800V HVDC之導線架為獨家供應商)，預期 2026 年 AI相關營收將倍數增
長，有機會單月營收貢獻超過 10%；在車用方面，受惠庫存調整結束，同時
電動車需求激增，預期全年呈雙位數增長；在均熱片方面，公司耕耘近兩年，
於今年 3月正式出貨美系網通大廠，此外公司均熱片亦切入美系記憶體大
廠，受惠今年記憶體市況火熱，將有機會進一步擴大營收規模，而更大尺寸
產品用於 CPU/GPU 產品將陸續驗證開始出貨，預計將高速成長至 2028 年。
在毛利率方面：受惠 26H1 漲價帶動，稼動率維持高檔，同時產品組合轉佳
下，將有助於本業持續改善。綜上所述，考量 H1財報優於預期，展望更趨
正向，兆豐投顧上修順德 2026 年營收 136.4 億元，YoY +33.1%，毛利率
18.8%，年增 5.5ppt，稅後淨利 11.9 億元，YoY +291.7%，EPS 6.5元(原
估 5.7元)。

期未來六個月股價跌幅超過-30%）。

產業供不應求將延續數季 
 2Q26 營收 825.4 億元(+68%QoQ)，ASP 季增超過六十位數百分比 ，
受惠於 ASP 大幅上揚且折舊費用持穩，毛利率 79.5%優於前季之
67.9%，營業利益 608.2 億元(+102%QoQ)優於預估值 47%，EPS 
14.66 元優於預期之 9.78 元。展望 3Q26，DRAM 產業供不應求持續 ，
惟部分客戶長約生效 ， 且消費性應用需求下修將壓抑合約價漲幅 ， 估
計 3Q26 營收 944.2 億元(+14.4%QoQ)，EPS 16.81 元。 
 AI 強勁需求將推動記憶體供需結構改變，降低景氣循環對於供應商
影響，預期 DRAM 產業供不應求將延續數季，3Q26 傳統型 DRAM
價格季增 13-18%。南亞科目前來自於 AI 營收超過兩成，DDR4 占
產出比重 60-70%，DDR5 和 DDR3 各占 10%，DDR5 比重未增加
主因在於產能不足以及客戶需求導致。 
 南亞科整體新廠投資規模最終將達產能 45K 以及金額 4800 億元 。 就
產能開出規劃而言，公司將新廠產能 1H28 20K 目標上修至 2028 年
30K。儘管資本支出積極，但透過提高長約占比降低營運風險，目前
長約占比已達 50%，未來將增加更多長約客戶。 
 預估 2026 年和 2027 年 EPS 分別為 55.98 元和 71.48 元。因 AI 降
低景氣循環影響 ， 產業供不應求將延續數季 ， 維持買進 ，TP 600 元。

個股報告

南 亞 科 為 利 基 型DRAM 製造
廠，2026 年資本支出將大增至
520 億元

ASP 季 增 幅 超 過 六 成 ，
2Q26EPS 14.66 元優於預期

DRAM 合約價漲幅收斂 ， 但營業
率將持續提升，估計 3Q26 EPS 
16.81 元

南亞科為利基型 DRAM 製造商，最大股東為台塑集團之南亞塑膠持有 29.28%
股權，主要競爭對手包含 Samsung、SK Hynix 、Micron 以及華邦電，目前月
產能為 6.5 萬片，20nm 和 1B 為主力生產製程。依產品終端應用區分，消費性
電子和 AI 分別占營收比重為 60%+和 20%+。公司將 2026 年銷售量年增雙位
數上修到年增 15-20%，因新廠產能需待 1H27 開出且存貨處於低水位，故銷
售量年增率不如去年亮眼。至於在資本支出方面， 2026 年資本支出由去年之
134 億元大幅增加至 520 億元，七成將用於新廠廠務設施，三成則將用於設備
採購 。 此外 ， 新廠將於1Q27 開始裝機 ，2H27 開始量產 ，2028 年達成產能 30K，
新廠全產能 45K 需投入 4800 億元，公司今年配發現金股利 1.35 元。

南亞科銷售量 YOY

Source：Company

2Q26 營收 825.4 億元(+68%QoQ)優於預期之 616.6 億元 ，ASP 季增超過六十
位數百分比，銷售量和匯率皆為季持平，受惠於 ASP 大幅上揚且折舊費用持
穩，毛利率 79.5%優於前季之 67.9%，營業利益 608.2 億元(+102%QoQ)優於
預估值 47%， 稅後淨利501.9 億元(+93%QoQ)，EPS 14.66 元優於預期之 9.78
元。

DRAM 產業供不應求持續，惟部分客戶長約生效，且消費性應用需求下修將壓

元富證券投資顧問股份有限公司 台北市敦化南路二段 97 號 19 樓 (02)2325-3299  (115)金管投顧新字第 009 號         Page 3 of 5 
個股報告

DRAM 供不應求預期將延續數
季以上，新產能規劃將搭配客戶
長約需求

新廠資本支出積極，但搭配提高
長約占比降低營運風險

維持買進，目標價 600 元

在產業需求方面，AI GPU/CPU/ASIC 驅動的 HBM、DDR5 和 LP DDR5 需求
大幅成長 ，AI 基礎建設則帶動 eSSD、SmartNIC 與 BMC 採用更高規格 DRAM
因應高速資料處理、傳輸和系統管理要求，至於非AI 應用產能受限，將有利於
高階市場發展。在產業供給方面，雖供應商整體擴充產能，但產能規劃將搭配
客戶長約需求，並提升高利潤產品比重。故以整體市況而言，AI 強勁需求將推
動記憶體供需結構改變 ， 降低景氣循環對於供應商影響 ， 預期DRAM 產業供不
應求將延續數季。

在 DRAM 報價走勢上 ， 原廠與部分客戶簽訂複數年長約 ， 價格上漲主要來自於
非長約客戶，且受到消費應應用需求下修以及高基期影響，集邦預估 3Q26 傳
統型 DRAM 價格季增 13-18%低於前季之 58-63%。 南亞科目前來自於AI 營收
超過兩成，客戶類型主要為 AI 基礎建設。若以產品類型區分，DDR4 占產出比
重 60-70%，DDR5 和 DDR3 各占 10%，DDR5 比重未增加主因在於產能不足
以及客戶需求導致。

1Q26-3Q26 DRAM 產品合約價漲幅預估  
合約價漲幅 1Q26 2Q26E 3Q26F
HBM混合DRAM Up 80-85% Up 53-58% Up 8-13%
傳統型DRAM Up 93-98% Up 58-63% Up 13-18%

Source：Trendforce

南亞科今年資本支出維持 520 億元，明年則將大幅提升至 2000 億元，整體新
廠投資規模最終將達產能 45K 以及金額 4800 億元。就產能開出規劃而言，南
亞科將新廠產能 1H28 20K 目標上修至 2028 年 30K。儘管資本支出積極，但
公司將透過提高長約占比降低營運風險，輝達、Google、微軟、英特爾和超微
皆為公司客戶 ， 目前長約占比已達50%， 涵蓋單季至多年期的合約 ， 除Kioxia、
Sandisk、SK Hynix 和 Cisco 四大私募客戶外，未來將增加更多長約客戶。此
外，隨獲利能力以及現金流皆轉趨強勁，儘管面臨高資本支出計畫，預期公司
股利配發率將達 40%。

以私募後股本 345 億元計算 ， 預估2026 年和 2027 年 EPS 分別為 55.98 元和
71.48 元。考量 AI 降低景氣循環影響，產業供不應求將延續數季，維持買進評
等，TP 600 元 (2027 PBR x 3.4)。

   公司簡介 
南亞科成立於 1995 年，為 DRAM 之研發、設計、製造與銷售商，於美國、歐洲、日
本、中國設立海外行銷據點，全球市占率約 1%，為台灣第一大 DRAM 製造商。營收
佔比方面，DDR3 約 20%、DDR4 約 70%、DDR5 約 10%。 
投資評等/目標價 重點摘要 
強力買進 750 
【 前 次 投 資 建 議 】強 力 買 進，565 
【大盤指數/股價】45355 / 435.5

➢ 2Q26 持續受惠合約價上漲，毛利率優於預期 
2Q26 營收 825.49 億元，QoQ+68.17%，YoY+684.23%，營收成長動
能主要仍來自於供需吃緊帶動 ASP 漲幅季增逾 60%，位元出貨量約略
與 1Q26 持平所致 。2Q26 毛利率 79.49%， 營業費用因研發與員工分紅
略 高 於 預 期 ， 惟 新 台 幣 貶 值 挹 注 匯 兌 利 益 ，EPS 為 14.66 元 ，
QoQ+92.6%。 
➢ 3Q26 ASP 漲幅可望逾 50%，營收獲利大幅增長 
展望 3Q26，預期 ASP 漲幅可望優於原廠漲幅，預估季增逾 50%，同
時，隨著1b 良率提升，3Q26 位元出貨量有望較 2Q26 微幅增加，整體
而言 ， 預估3Q26 營收約 1,255.56 億元 ，QoQ+52.1%，YoY+568.6%，
毛利率約 86.57%，EPS 為 24.15 元，QoQ+66.03%，YoY+5,230.47%。  
➢ 2H26 DDR4 16G 貢獻營收，2027 年新廠開出，營運持續升溫 
展望 2026 年，考量 3Q26 ASP 有望大幅優於投研部先前預期，且上漲
動能將至少延續至 2027 年，此外，位元出貨量預估約年增 20%，上調
2026 年營收至約 3,980 億元 ，YoY+497.74%， 毛利率83.24%，EPS 為
74.71 元，YoY+3,747%。 
展望 2027 年，新廠機台預估 2Q27 開始投片，最快將於 4Q27 開始貢
獻營收，屆時月產能預計將較 2026 年新增 2 萬片。需求端方面，單機
DRAM 搭載量的增加將使得總需求量持續成長，整體產業仍將處於供不
應求市況當中 ， 預期ASP 仍有望小幅度上漲 。 預估2027 年營收約 6,465
億元 ，YoY+62.43%， 毛利率87.49%，EPS 為 126.45 元，YoY+71.49%。  
➢ 2H26 DDR4 16G 貢獻營收，2027 年新廠開出，營運持續升溫 
評價方面，考量先前愷俠、SANDISK、SOLIDIGM 等入股南亞科，客戶
結構出現改變且訂單需求強勁，將有助擺脫過往傳統景氣循環影響，且
預估 2027 年 DDR5 佔比將逐步提升。 看好隨著AI 發展 ， 記憶體已經上
升至重要戰略資源，同時短期供給端難以出現大量新產能，南亞科是少
數於 2027 年可以擴產並貢獻營收的記憶體廠，再加上公司正與多個客
戶進行客製化 DRAM 的合作 ， 有助提升長線獲利能見度 ， 故投研部改以
本益比進行評價，維持強力買進建議，目標價 750 元(2026F PE 10X)。

個股報告

公司簡介 
南亞科成立於 1995 年，為 DRAM(動態隨機存取記憶體)之研發、設計、製造與銷售
商，於美國、歐洲、日本、中國設立海外行銷據點，全球市占率約1%，為全球第五大
DRAM 製造商、台灣第一大 DRAM 製造商，1Q26 競爭同業市占分別為 SK 海力士
(000660 KS) 28.8%、三星(005930 KS) 38.5%、美光(MU US)22.4%；DRAM 上游的
生產材料為矽晶圓，南亞科主要向台勝科(3532)購買；DRAM 下游的封裝與測試則由
福懋科(8131)進行，南亞科主要股東為台塑集團的南亞塑膠，營收佔比方面， DDR3
約 20%、DDR4 約 70%、DDR5 約 10%。應用別方面，Server+PC 約 20%~25%，
eSSD 約 10%，車用與工業約 10%，消費性產品&其他約 55%。 
2Q26 持續受惠合約價上漲，毛利率優於預期 
2Q26 營收 825.49 億元，QoQ+68.17%，YoY+684.23%，營收成長動能主要仍來自
於供需吃緊帶動 ASP 漲幅季增逾 60%，並且位元出貨量約略與 1Q26 持平所致。獲
利表現方面，2Q26 毛利率 79.49%，QoQ+11.62ppts，YoY+100.06ppts，優於預
期，主要仍受惠於 ASP 上漲所帶動、1b 製程良率提升以及 DDR4 8Gb 營收占比持續
提升優化產品組合所致。營業費用雖然因研發費用與員工分紅等支出略高於預期，惟
新台幣貶值挹注匯兌利益，2Q26 稅後淨利為 501.92 億元，QoQ+92.6%，EPS 為
14.66 元。產品組合方面，考量當前 DDR4/LPDDR4 供給極為有限，公司仍將出貨重
心擺在 DDR4/LPDDR4，故出貨占比約 7 成。應用領域方面，包含車用、工業及部分
通訊的廣義消費型電子產品佔比超過 60%，AI 基礎設施與伺服器相關的營收佔比則
超過 20%。 
3Q26 ASP 漲幅可望逾 50%，營收獲利大幅增長 
從整體 DRAM 市場來看，市場的主要變化仍在於 AI 應用帶動的結構性轉變正在減緩
過去記憶體市場明顯的週期性波動，需求端正經歷由 B2C 向 B2B 轉移的現象。生成
式 AI 的硬體架構推升了 GPU、CPU、TPU 及 ASIC 對 HBM、LPDDR5 及 DDR5 元
件的使用量。同時，AI 基礎設施也增加了企業級 SSD、智慧網卡與 BMC 等採用較大
容量與高效能 DRAM 的比例。在供給端方面，AI 伺服器相關記憶體產能的增加，排
擠了部分智慧型手機 、 個人電腦及一般消費性電子的記憶體供給額度 。 非AI 應用領域
在產能受限的情況下，市場供應轉向偏好高階產品型號，導致終端市場出現定價結構
的調整。整體而言，短期內 B2B 的強勁需求與供給瓶頸仍難以改變，預期 3Q26 原廠
DRAM 報價漲幅可望季增逾 30%。 
展望 3Q26，預期營運動能將延續，獲利表現可望持續優化。在 ASP 方面，考量部分
客戶仍有換約需求，合約價具調漲空間，以及 DDR4 8Gb 為與原廠報價切齊，預估
3Q26 南亞科 ASP 漲幅可望優於原廠漲幅，投研部預估季增逾 50%，同時，隨著 1b
良率提升，預期 3Q26 位元出貨量有望較 2Q26 微幅增加，整體而言，預估 3Q26 營
收約 1,255.56 億元， QoQ+52.1% ， YoY+568.6% ，毛利率約 86.57% ，
QoQ+7.08ppts ， YoY+68.12ppts ， 稅 後 淨 利 948.55 億 元 ， QoQ+66.03% ，
YoY+5,230.47%，EPS 為 24.15 元。 
2H26 DDR4 16G 貢獻營收，2027 年新廠開出，營運持續升溫 
展望 2026 年，隨著原廠 DDR4 陸續 EOL，預期 2H26 DDR4 供需缺口將進一步放
大，尤其工控、網通等客戶仍停留在DDR4，並且以 16Gb DDR4 和 LPDDR4 需求尤
為強勁 。 南亞科目前持續優化產能提供DDR4 的需求 ， 並且維持原先1H26 送樣 16Gb 
DDR4，2H26 貢獻營收的計畫。綜上所述，考量 3Q26 ASP 有望大幅優於投研部先

### Page 3

- 3 -

個股報告 
前預期，且上漲動能將至少延續至 2027 年，此外 ，位元出貨量方面 ， 投研部預估2026
年位元出貨量約年增 20%，故上調 2026 年營收至約 3,980 億元，YoY+497.74%，
毛利率 83.24%，YoY+60.73ppts，除了受惠 ASP 上漲及良率提升外，全年折舊亦有
下滑加上 DDR4 16GB 開始貢獻營收優化產品組合，共同推升獲利表現，稅後淨利
2,544.4 億元，YoY+3747%，EPS 為 74.71 元。 
展望 2027 年，公司表示新廠機台將於 1Q27 移入，預估 2Q27 開始投片，最快將於
4Q27 開始貢獻營收，屆時月產能預計將較 2026 年新增 2 萬片，全年位元年成長率
逾 10%。需求端方面，edge AI 與 AI PC 的發展預期將成為改變 DRAM 搭載量的重
要成長動能，如支援地端推論的 AI PC 主記憶體需求有機會達 128GB，而雲端伺服器
單一 CPU 推論模組的記憶體配置需求亦達 1.5TB，硬體規格的升級將對記憶體市場總
量產生額外需求，故當智慧型手機或傳統個人電腦的總銷量增長放緩時， 單機DRAM
搭載量的增加將使得總需求量持續成長，整體產業仍將處於供不應求市況當中，預期
ASP 仍有望小幅度上漲。預估 2027 年營收約 6465 億元，YoY+62.43%，毛利率
87.49%，YoY+4.25ppts， 稅後淨利約4,363 億元 ，YoY+71.49%，EPS 為 126.45 元。 
DRAM 成為 AI 成長重要資源，長線獲利能見度提升，維持強力買進 
整體而言，隨著原廠陸續將產能轉往更高階的 HBM 及 DDR5，而需求面仍有工控、
網通以及消費性客戶採用 DDR4，供需緊張態勢將至少延續至 2027 年，乃至 2028
年，仍看好 DRAM 合約價將呈現上行趨勢。針對市場對於 2027 年或 2028 年同業新
產能開出可能導致供需失衡的疑慮 ， 若將伺服器供應商宣告的擴產規模換算為EUV 設
備的實質產能增幅，整體的產能擴張幅度仍處於業界常態範圍內，加上多數新建產能
已具備 LTA 的基礎 ， 因此推估市場在未來兩年不致出現供過於求 。 為應對中長期需求 ，
南亞科總投資額 160 億美元的新廠建置計畫按既定進度執行 ， 目標為打造每月4.5 萬
片的產能，新廠初期的製程規劃將優先採用現有良率穩定的 16 奈米產線設定，以確
保能即時生產 DDR4 或 DDR5 產品，同步為下一代 1C 製程的量產爭取技術調校時
間。投研部看好隨著 Agentic AI 需求增加，以及 AI 伺服器對於 eSSD 的用量提升，
將持續加劇 DRAM 供需緊張態勢；另中國記憶體供給增加方面，投研部預期其主要
受惠對象僅為中國境內 PC、 手機等受到伺服器需求排擠的消費性電子客戶 ， 而綜觀全
球需求，伺服器已經成為 DRAM 最大的需求來源，並且隨著 CPU 的使用量增加，將
進一步推升 DRAM 需求量，故投研部認為中國即便增加供給，仍對於整體供需影響
相對有限。 
在技術發展與新產品開發進度上，南亞科的 1c、1d、1e 製程節點以及 EUV 技術導入
計畫皆按既定時程推進。其中，1c 預計於 2026 年底至 1Q27 進入準備階段，並將逐
步進行良率學習與產品驗證。在產品線方面，公司維持產品組合的廣度，持續提供涵
蓋 DDR3、DDR4、LPDDR4 及 DDR5 與 LPDDR5 產品。目前公司具備 16Gb 產品的
量產能力，以供應伺服器 BMC、企業級 SSD 等應用的需求。同時，透過 2Q26 完成
的私募案，公司與包含企業級 SSD 及網通設備在內的國際客戶建立了至少三年的長
期合作與供應承諾，將成為支持公司產品線發展的基礎。 
評價方面，考量當前 DRAM 報價漲勢仍然強勁且優於預期，加上 2026 年折舊下滑、
良率增加帶動毛利率表現，預期獲利、營運動能將強於往年循環。此外，考量先前愷
俠、SANDISK、SOLIDIGM 等入股南亞科，為確保 eSSD 在 DDR4 的貨源充足，故
客戶結構出現改變且訂單需求強勁 ， 將有助擺脫過往傳統景氣循環影響 ， 且預估2027
年 DDR5 佔比將逐步提升。投研部看好隨著 AI 發展，記憶體已經上升至重要戰略資
源，同時考量短期供給端難以出現大量新產能，南亞科是少數於 2027 年可以擴產並

### Page 4

- 4 -

個股報告 
貢獻營收的記憶體廠，再加上公司正與多個客戶進行客製化 DRAM 的合作，其中亦
包含 WoW 產品，有助提升長線獲利能見度，故投研部改以本益比進行評價，維持強
力買進建議，目標價 750 元(2026F PE 10X)。

營收走勢圖

研究員/鄭高輯 OWENCHENG@uni-psg.com

結論： 
南亞科 2Q26 持續繳出亮眼的成績，GM 及 OPM 分別達到 79.5%及
73.7%的水準，不亞於一線大廠，單季稅後 EPS 14.66 元，符合上
修後預期。公司預期3Q26 營運及 OPM 將進一步提升，且短缺情況
至 4Q26 未見變化，同時不預期市場在 2028 年前會發生反轉。然
而，Trendforce 預估 3Q26 一般型 Dram 價格漲幅 13~18%，較2Q26
的 58~63%明顯收斂，顯示儘管雲端客戶對於漲價接受度仍高，但
終端市場價格承受能力已明顯減弱，而南亞科 non-AI 佔比仍有
80%。在投資建議上，預估2026 及 2027 年稅後 EPS 分別為 61.84
元及 93.37 元，儘管目前評價偏低(明年 PER 5X)，但DDR4 長期市
場萎縮的趨勢難以避免，同時價格漲幅持續減緩，故維持中立建
議。

重點摘要:

1.南亞科 2Q26 GM 及 OPM 亮眼，單季稅後 EPS 14.66 元: 
南亞科 2Q26 營收+68.2%QoQ，主要係ASP 季增率>60％所帶動，銷
售量及匯率則持平 。 毛利率持續受惠報價上漲 ， 較前季大增11.6pt
至 79.5%，符合上修後預期的79.2%。儘管費用率因營收提升而略
降 0.7pt 至 5.8%，但員工分紅增加，使得降幅較預期小。單季稅
後 EPS 14.66 元，大致符合上修後預期的 14.34 元。

2.DDR4 佔比近 70%，短期未規劃提升 DDR5 比重： 
在產品組合方面，DDR4 與 LPDDR4 合計占比近 70%，DDR5、DDR3 及
其他各約佔 10%。公司短期並未規劃提升 DDR5 比重，主要係公司
沒有多餘產能，同時DDR4 供應商有限，因此生產主力仍會集中在
DDR4。應用面而言，上半年雲端伺服器及eSSD(搭載 DDR4)應用佔
比約 20%，消費應用佔 60%，其餘為 PC 及手機。

3.3Q26 營運將進一步提升，然研調預估漲幅明顯收斂： 
展望 2H26， 公司預期3Q26 營運將進一步提升 ， 即使OPM 已經相當
高，但將隨著報價持續上漲而進一步提升，4Q26 需視市場變化而
定，目前並未看到太大改變。

### Page 2

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  2

公司並未提供 3Q26 報價預估，不過根據 Trendforce 預估，3Q26
一般型 Dram 價格漲幅 13~18%，較2Q26 的 58~63%明顯收斂，儘管
雲端伺服器對記憶體需求未減 ， 但消費市場對價格抗性已經減弱 。
預估 2026 全年稅後 EPS 61.84 元。

4.長約確保 2027 年營運穩健，不預期 2028 年前產業發生反轉： 
展望 2027 年，僅管記憶體大廠有較多擴產計畫，但公司認為換算
成產能增加後仍在合理範圍內， 不預期市場在2028 年前會發生反
轉。隨著長期合約(LTA)比重增加，預期價格將趨於穩定，公司多
數長約是預先約定成交量，並考慮在長約中加入價格下限或現金
預付 ， 預期未來數季營運將維持穩定 。 由於新廠第一階段產能3 萬
片/月要到 2028 年量產，因此 2027 年銷售成長率仍平穩。預估
2027 年稅後 EPS 93.37 元。

研 究  報 告                
立隆電(2472.TT) 
產業：電子–電子零組件 
車用電子及 AI 帶動產品組合轉佳，獲利逐漸回升  
 投資評價與建議 
考量到立隆電打入美系客戶供應鏈，且為日廠外唯⼀擁有松下專利權的製造
商，帶動其產業地位的提升，惟AI占比仍低，評價受壓，基於2027年EPS計算，
目前預期本益比約23倍，位於合理區間，建議逢低佈局。 
 重點評論分析 
1Q26 受車用電子與工業需求， 帶動營收季增 9.2%至 29.4 億元 ：立隆電 2Q26
營收季增 25%至 36.82 億元，主要受惠於產品組合優化及匯率友善，獲利部
分，雖 1Q26 受到成本影響，但預估毛利率將回升至 32.3%，EPS 2.81 元。 
以車用為主，AI為輔的成⻑動能：展望未來，泰國新廠預期4Q26放量，因應非
中客戶，月產能將從6億增加至6.39億顆，而成⻑動能⾸重⾞用電⼦，新⾞市場
銷售雖較為溫和 ， 但隨新能源⾞的滲透率持續提升 ， 將帶動鋁電容的用量增加 ，
以新能源⾞鋁電容用量約為傳統⾞的4倍推估，2026年有望貢獻約52億元，
2027年來到64億元，其次則是AI相關產品，新切入美系AI供應鏈，2H26將逐
步放量，新產品用量推估為舊產品的5倍，預期至4Q26占比可提升至5%，惟公
司因風險分散策略，單⼀客戶比重較低，使得營收貢獻相對受限，獲利方面，
觀察到AI需求強勁，使上游的高階鋁箔供應緊繃，產業逐漸從成本轉嫁轉向需
求推動，而緊缺恐延續至2027年，預計3Q26漲價將逐漸發酵，加上AI與⾞用
帶動產品組合優化，我們認為2H26營運將優於1H26，2027年將持續向上。 
 投資風險 
⾞用電⼦需求不如預期、AI產品時程遞延。

| 公司簡介  
立隆電(2472)為台灣鋁電容大廠，汽車為主要應用：立隆電成立於1976年，為台灣鋁電解電容大廠，產品專注於
液態 、 固態及固液混合的鋁電解電容 ， 占比約65% ， 鋁箔與其他則占35% ， 旗下擁有持股約30%的⼦公司立敦(6175) ，
負責上游材料的供應，在終端應用上，立隆電主要以高毛利的⾞用占比約36%，其餘如工業設備、消費電⼦、電
信網通、雲端資訊及代理商分別約18%、8%、8%、4%以及26%。

 圖表、終端應用占比

資料來源：立隆電 
| 產業趨勢  
各電容產品有適合的容值及電壓：以各種產品的特性來看，陶瓷電容的產品選擇擁有較廣的容量與耐壓區間，較
具通用性，而聚合物鋁電解電容與鉭電容則集中於高電容量區域，專攻電源濾波與大電流穩定需求，較常應用在
低頻的環境，且因鉭電容造價昂貴，但耐熱度高，因此較常運用在⾞用以及航太軍工上，其餘像薄膜電容較常運
用在交流電中，矽電容與可變電容等特殊元件，則定位在特定電性需求的利基市場。 
圖表、電容種類比較

資料來源：村田 
| 營運概況  
2Q26 受惠於產品組合優化，帶動營收季增 25%至 36.8 億元：立隆電 2Q26 營收季增 25%至 36.82 億元，截至 6
月營收年增 18%至 66 億，主要受惠於產品組合優化及匯率友善，獲利方面，因原物料成本將逐漸轉移至定價上，
預估毛利率於 1Q26 見底，2Q26 回升至 32.3%，EPS 2.81 元。

### Page 3

3 
以車用為主，AI 為輔的成⻑動能，帶動整體營運：展望未來，立隆電的成⻑動能將關注在：(1)⾞用業務：觀察整體
⾞市銷售較為疲弱，但根據公司表示，隨新能源⾞的滲透率持續提升，將帶動鋁電容的用量增加，以新能源⾞鋁
電容用量約為傳統⾞的 4 倍之預估，2026 年有望貢獻約 52 億元，2027 年來到 64 億元，(2)AI 伺服器產品中，
主要應用在運算板上的固液混合電容以及固態電容(對標松下 SP-CAP 產品) ， 目前已切入美系客戶 ， 客戶新產品用
量較前代增加約 5 倍，2Q26 已有少量出貨，預期於 3Q26 開始放量，將延續至 2027 年，且公司為非日系廠商
中，目前唯⼀擁有松下專利權的製造商，這使得美系 AI 客戶在選擇非日系供應商時，有望因法律合規性而優先採
用公司產品，目前 AI 占比約 2%，預期 4Q26 可提升至 5%，但貢獻相對有限，因此，預計成⻑動能仍以⾞用電
⼦的成⻑為主 ， AI 產品為輔 ， 產能部分 ， 目前惠州以及蘇州廠月產能合計約 6 億顆 ， 泰國新廠預期在 4Q26 放量 ，
主要是地緣政治風險，以因應部分非中客戶要求，月產能將增加至 6.39 億顆。 
在原物料方面，除了上游原材料成本攀升外，根據公司表示，目前 AI 伺服器需求上升，使高階鋁箔開始有供不應
求的趨勢，市場開始轉成賣方市場，而公司擁有⼦公司立敦確保原物料供應，目前已與客戶協商談價，隨新舊合
約的交替，預期 3Q26 起逐漸發酵，推動 2H26 獲利表現顯著優於 1H26，而高階材料供需吃緊態勢也將隨 AI 伺
服器新產品放量，有望延續至 2027 年，基於以上，預估 2026/2027 年營收將年增 27.8%/22.1%至 144/176 億
元，毛利率提升至 32.5%/34.6%，EPS 達 10.7 元/14.8 元。

考量到立隆電目前打入美系客戶供應鏈，且為非日廠中，擁有松下專利權的製造商，帶動其產業地位的提升，且
漲價從成本逐漸轉變成需求推動，參考過往區間以及同業(日廠及金山電等)評價，基於 2027 年 EPS 計算，目前
預期本益比約 23 倍，位於合理區間，給予 25 倍之本益比。

2548.TT 華固 
 住宅廠辦並進降低政策影響 目前評價低

[財務資訊更新]   
   公司簡介 
華固建設股份有限公司成立於 1989 年 4 月 6 日，原是以北部地區商辦和住宅建案為
主的建商，後期開始轉向北部的地上權、以及中南部的精華地區發展。公司主要營業
項目為委託營造廠商興建住宅、商辦及工業廠房出售，以及部分出租業務。  
投資評等/目標價 重點摘要 
買進 132 
【 前 次 投 資 建 議 】買進，150 
【大盤指數/股價】42671 / 101

➢ 2026 預期營收維持相對高檔，打房政策暫無影響 
展望 2026 年，預計將有三個建案完工入帳，分別為《華固上文林》 ，以
及《華固一莊》 、 《華固時代置地》 ， 《華固上文林》截至五月已經交屋85
戶，2H26 的交屋重心將轉向總銷約 41 億元的 《華固一莊》 ； 《華固時代
置地》總銷 78 億元，該案已完工並取得使照，目標於 2H26 交屋認列。
整體而言，投研部預期 2026 年營收約 176.5 億元，YoY-3.2%。毛利率
27.73%，稅後淨利 33.91 億元，YoY+4.62%，EPS 為 10.58 元。  
➢ 2027 年三案完工，總銷約 205 億元，將於 2H27 開始交屋認列 
展望 2027 年 ， 預計有位於台中 、 總銷108 億元的 《華固頂匯》 、 總銷47
億元 《華固豐匯》 以及新北三重 《華固慕川》 總銷55 億元入帳。雖然台
中房市受到打房政策影響，短期銷售率略有停滯，但隨市場逐步消化，
以及觀察近期政府打房政策並未進一步緊縮，且預期短期內政府並不會
端出新的限制性政策，再加上新青安 3.0 推出，看好後續買氣將逐漸回
溫，銷售率仍有望持續提升。預估 2027 年營收約為 178.22 億元，
YoY+0.95%，毛利率 28.32%，EPS 為 11.03 元，YoY+4.48%。 
➢ 公司業績能見度高，營運穩健成長 
華固個案推出時程能見度已到 2034 年，並保持每年有建案完工認列的
進度，後續建案包含預售個案以及儲備個案總銷約 1,750 億元，儲備個
案方面，公司於 2Q26 法說會上新增央北 2 案以及泰山新貴案，達成
2026 年購置三塊土地的目標。整體而言，公司建案業績能見度高，並且
商辦與住宅並進，減少房市波動對於公司營運的影響，預期營運將穩健
成長。 
➢ 預期政策不再緊縮，目前評價低，維持買進建議 
評價方面，預估 2026 年每股淨值為 73.32 元，過往 PB 區間約為
1.3X~2.8X，目前 1.37X，位處區間下緣，評價相對較低，同時除了考量
公司業績能見度高之外，在當前整體房市位於谷底之際，公司於 2026
年、2027 年皆有廠辦大樓完工，可望大幅降低房市交投清淡的影響，再
加上其具高殖利率題材 ， 故對其長線營運維持看好，維持買進投資建議 ，
目標價 132 元(2026F PB 1.8X)。

個股報告

公司簡介 
華固建設自 1989 年成立以來，已確立其為台灣營建業領導地位，公司策略主要深耕
雙北地區 ， 近年成功將開發版圖延伸至台中精華地段如水湳經貿園區 。 在營運模式上 ，
華固採取住宅與頂級商辦廠辦並進的雙軌策略，以此平衡房地產市場波動風險並創造
穩定收益。此外，華固過往均維持穩定的高現金股利配發政策，在未來業績能見度高
的前提下，具備殖利率題材。 
1Q26《華固上文林》交屋入賬 
1Q26 總銷 61 億元的《華固上文林》開始交屋入賬，2026 年預計共將交屋 88 戶，
其中約七成於 1Q26 已經完成交屋，故 1Q26 營收約 42.06 億元，QoQ-68.55%，
YoY+36,624%，獲利表現方面，由於《華固上文林》2024 年銷售率即逾九成，故雖
然輝達進駐北市科，為未能顯著反映於《華固上文林》房價上，然預期後續《華固芙
心》可望明顯受惠，1Q26 毛利率約 26%，QoQ-2.22ppts，YoY-66.01ppts，稅後淨
利 7.27 億元，EPS 為 2.28 元。 
2026 預期營收維持相對高檔，打房政策暫無影響 
06/2026 營收 2.25 億元，MoM-29%，YoY-88.8%，略低於預期，推測約有 2~3 戶
交屋進度將遞延至 3Q26，2Q26 營收 15.01 億元，QoQ-64.3%，YoY-25.56%，預
估毛利率為 26.3%，稅後淨利 2.8 億元，EPS 為 0.88 元。 
展望 2026 年 ， 預計將有三個建案完工入帳 ， 分別為前段所述 《華固上文林》， 以及《華
固一莊》、《華固時代置地》 ，雖然2026 年仍受到政府打房政策影響，房市偏冷，然而
《華固上文林》 、 《華固一莊》皆為2022 年所開案，且皆為台北地區，供給相對較緊
缺，同時 《華固上文林》 又位處北士科，故皆已完銷，並未受到政府打房影響。其中，
《華固上文林》截至五月已經交屋 85 戶，2H26 的交屋重心將轉向總銷約 41 億元的
《華固一莊》 ，目前該案已取得使用執照並進入對保與交屋程序，預計於 3Q26 開始
認列營收，為 2H26 業績提供實質支撐； 《華固時代置地》總銷78 億元，為位於大直
的商辦大樓，該案已完工並取得使照，經營團隊目前以全棟銷售為優先考量，並與潛
在買家洽談中 ，若評估全棟成交機率降低，將隨時啟動分層銷售作業 ，公司目標於
2H26 交屋認列。整體而言，三個建案總銷約 184 億元，不過考量《華固時代置地》
可能折價售予單一大客戶，故投研部預期 2026 年營收約 176.5 億元，YoY-3.2%。獲
利表現方面，雖然《華固上文林》、《華固一莊》毛利率約於過往 25%~30%區間中下
緣，惟考量部分建案接待中心已於 2025 年認列費用，預估 2026 年毛利率仍有中緣
之上水準約 27.73%，稅後淨利 33.91 億元，YoY+4.62%，EPS 為 10.58 元。 
2027 年三案完工，總銷約 205 億元，將於 2H27 開始交屋認列 
展望 2027 年，預計有位於台中、總銷 108 億元的《華固頂匯》 、總銷47 億元《華固
豐匯》以及新北三重《華固慕川》總銷 55 億元入帳，其中，《華固頂匯》為華固進軍
台中的第一塊敲門磚，該案分兩期共 260 戶，預計於全案將於 2027 年初完工，第一
期 200 戶目前已經完銷，第二期預計將於 2026 年年底開始銷售，其銷售單價可望高
於第一階段。《華固豐匯》由於開案時間相對較晚，且適逢政府打房期間，故當前銷售
率呈現緩慢提升，截至 2Q26 約 35%-40%，公司期望透過 《華固頂匯》的優異表現，
帶動《華固豐匯》銷售率的提升。整體而言，台中為 2027 年華固主要營收來源，雖
然台中房市受到打房政策影響，短期銷售率略有停滯，但隨市場逐步消化，以及觀察
近期政府打房政策並未進一步緊縮，且預期短期內政府並不會端出新的限制性政策，

### Page 3

- 3 -

個股報告 
再加上新青安 3.0 推出，看好後續買氣將逐漸回溫，銷售率仍有望持續提升。《華固慕
川》目前銷售率約 95%，幾近完銷，預估完工時間約落在 3Q26，並可望於 2H26 認
列營收。整體而言，預估 2027 年完工金額約為 205 億元，然考量《華固豐匯》恐未
能於 2027 年完銷，投研部預估 2027 年營收約為 178.22 億元，YoY+0.95%，獲利
表現方面，受惠於《華固頂匯》及《華固慕川》推案時間適逢買氣熱絡階段，售價相
對較高，故毛利率 28.32%，YoY+0.58ppts，稅後淨利 35.43 億元，YoY+4.48%，
EPS 為 11.03 元。 
公司業績能見度高，營運穩健成長 
展望後市，華固的個案推出時程能見度已到 2034 年，期間陸續有新案推出，並保持
每年有建案完工認列的進度 ， 後續建案包含預售個案以及儲備個案總銷約1,750 億元 。
2028 年總銷 328 億元的《華固創匯園區》完工，公司預計 2028 年達到 50%交屋，
其餘五成則觀察 2029 年、2030 年輝達入駐後 ， 是否將進一步帶動周邊房價再陸續售
出。而在中長期方面，建案規模最大的為位於新店 「正大新店案」，該案為與正大尼龍
集團合建之項目，基地總面積高達 1.3 萬餘坪，並涵蓋未來的捷運南環線出口，全區
預計將興建 10 至 12 棟建築 ， 可售總銷金額高達518 億元 。 該案預計於2027 年取得
建照並展開為期約一年的整地工程，計畫於 2028 年正式搭建接待中心並啟動銷售，
將成為未來營收成長的重要動能。儲備個案方面，公司於 1Q26 法說會上新增松勇路
案以及安和都更案，預計分別於 2030 年以及 2034 年完工交屋，於 2Q26 法說會上
新增央北 2 案以及泰山新貴案，達成 2026 年購置三塊土地的目標。整體而言，公司
建案業績能見度高，並且商辦與住宅並進，減少房市波動對於公司營運的影響，再搭
配更長線的都更案，預期營運將穩健成長。

個股報告 
預期政策不再緊縮，目前評價低，維持買進建議 
觀察到政府近期針對新青安 3.0 政策所做出的調整，如設立所得限制、總價上限、排
富條款等，顯示出政策核心在於防堵投機炒作與防範搭便車行為，但實質上依然支持
具備換屋需求的自住客群。在當前不動產放款集中度已見下降且房價趨於穩定的情勢
下，預期政府短期內將維持政策現狀，不至於再端出更加嚴苛的限制性措施，故整體
房市應已大致見底。評價方面，預估 2026 年每股淨值為 73.32 元，過往 PB 區間約
為 1.3X~2.8X，目前 1.37X，位處區間下緣，評價相對較低，同時除了考量公司業績
能見度高，以及建案多集中大台北地區，已上線的推案銷售率相對較高之外，在當前
政府實施信用管制尚未鬆綁、整體房市位於谷底之際，公司於 2026 年、2027 年皆有
廠辦大樓完工，分別位於大直以及北市科，可望大幅降低房市交投清淡的影響，再加
上其具高殖利率題材，故對其長線營運維持看好，惟考量公司甫於 6/2 除息，短期不
易吸引資金進駐，投研部維持買進投資建議，目標價 132 元(2026F PB 1.8X)。

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What's new: We share our latest marketing feedback from our Singapore 
and Malaysia trips during the week of 6–10 July.

What's the impact: Investors remains constructive on AI. Since our 
previous US marketing trip, mainstream AI names have corrected by c.30% 
over the past 2–3 weeks. However, based on discussions with investors, 
most remain positive on the AI cycle and related hardware names. The 
pullback was largely viewed as positioning-driven, given how crowded the 
names had become. Fundamentally, we believe the supply-demand 
imbalance is becoming more evident across the supply chain. While we 
see substrates offering the greatest near-term pricing upside among 
shortage-driven components, we also note that power semiconductors 
have recently joined the shortage bucket.

Investment thesis: price hike > spec upgrade. As agentic AI and 
inference demand gain traction, AI spending is likely to shift gradually 
towards ASICs and CPU servers from 2026, away from Nvidia GPU-led 
capex in 2023–2025. While total AI spending will likely continue to rise, 
second-order content growth driven by spec upgrades may moderate as 
spending mix shifts from training to inference. We remain constructive on 
the ongoing server spec-upgrade cycle, but we think earnings upside from 
price hikes may be more meaningful from 2H26 to 2027. In our view, much 
of the savings from the mix shift towards ASIC/CPU servers will be 
absorbed by shortage components such as memory and substrates. 
Accordingly, we believe investors should pay close attention to the 
substrate industry, where pricing momentum has recently accelerated. We 
expect a wave of earnings upgrades, similar to what we have seen for 
memory vendors from 2H25. Separately, while CPU server shipments may 
face some risk in 2026, given component shortages, long-only investors we 
met remained constructive on CPU-related names over the long term.

What we recommend: We remain positive on Taiwan data centre names, 
given the recent read-through from ongoing component shortages. That 
said, we are more positive on price-hike beneficiaries, which could see 
stronger earnings revisions than names benefiting mainly from spec 
upgrades, especially when shipment assumptions might face downside risk 
and AI spending shifts towards relatively low-end servers. We also believe 
cost savings from the mix shift will be offset by higher component prices. 
Against this backdrop, we turn more positive on the substrate sector and 
continue to prefer NYPCB (8046 TT, Buy [1]) as our top idea.

How we differ: We set out our current view on how ASEAN-based 
investors are positioned towards the Taiwan data centre hardware sector.

Taiwan Data Centre Hardware

Key takeaways from the SG/KL marketing trip

➢ Investors remain constructive on AI theme; are blaming the recent 
pullback on the positioning issue 
➢ Component shortage turns fiercer in the short term; we expect price 
hike stories to draw most investors’ attention  
➢ We pick substrate/NYPCB as our top ideas on highest earnings 
upward revisions; Delta remains the name attracting highest queries

  Taiwan Data Centre Hardware: 13 July 2026 
ASEAN marketing key takeaways 
Key feedback from investors  
Constructive view remains 
Despite the c.30% pullback in share prices from the recent peak during our US marketing 
trip in the week of 22 June, we believe sentiment remains constructive. Of the investors we 
met, 70-80% remained positive on the current AI cycle and on stock performance. Most 
viewed the recent correction as primarily driven by positioning concerns rather than a 
deterioration in fundamentals. Importantly, we did not encounter meaningful bearish views 
pointing to a capex bubble or a sharp slowdown from here. Many valuation-sensitive 
investors said they would look to add back on a meaningful pullback.

We believe the positive stance remains anchored in recent developments across the AI 
supply chain.

Short-term updates 
Component shortages appear to be worsening, as evidenced by more visible price hikes in 
ABF/BT substrates over the past 2–3 weeks. We also note that the global ABF leader has 
indicated an intention to exit the BT substrate market amid tight manufacturing capacity, 
driven by rising CPU demand and resilient AI server demand. In addition, TSMC foundry 
capacity and power semis, including MOSFETs and PMICs, remain in shortage. Based on 
our supply chain feedback, power semis have been massively mentioned over the past 1 
month (vs. little from 3 months ago).

Investors continue to focus on which subsector offers the best risk-reward profile within the 
price-hike theme. We currently prefer substrates, given the recent pricing momentum and 
the clear supply-demand imbalance. We expect substrates to follow a similar price hike 
pattern as we saw in memory from 2H25, despite a lower magnitude from substrates. We 
expect the substrate sector to deliver the strongest price hikes among the key shortage 
stories, with the price hike trend potentially extending through 2027.

That said, long-term concerns remain.

Long-term concerns 
As highlighted in our previous US marketing note on 30 June (Key takeaways from the US 
marketing trip), we believe capex from the top-4 hyperscalers is already nearing a 
constraint, with incremental increases unlikely to be funded solely by operating cash flow. 
Any further upside may need to come from equity/debt financing or VC funding. ASEAN 
investors are also concerned about the pace of AI monetisation, though the issue is more 
about construction capacity failing to keep up with token demand growth rather than 
demand weakness.

Our view remains unchanged. While investors are aware of these concerns, AI remains a 
highly disruptive technology, making it difficult to draw a clear line on capex sustainability. 
From our marketing feedback, Singapore investors appear less focused on the capex 
outlook, while Malaysia-based investors want more clarity on the broader picture and 
timing. We think this reflects a desire to trade around the theme tactically, even as they 
remain constructive on AI.

Kyber delay remains a key discussion topic 
Most investors do not expect Kyber to launch alongside Rubin in 2027, and ASEAN 
investors were largely neutral on whether Kyber arrives in 2028. We think it is still too early 
to conclude, as there are real technical bottlenecks, including the orthogonal mid-plane. 
That said, we remain confident Nvidia will work through these issues, given the importance 
of sustaining its moat in AI. 
We believe most 
investors maintain a 
constructive view on AI; 
short-term pullback 
should be more from 
positioning issue

### Page 4

4 
  Taiwan Data Centre Hardware: 13 July 2026

As the market moves towards agentic AI and inference, mid-to-low-end compute, including 
CPU servers, should gain importance. Still, Nvidia will likely remain dominant in AI training, 
thanks to its stronger compute performance. With Rubin Ultra’s die count already revised 
down from four to two per chip, rack-level power density would otherwise remain broadly 
flat from GB200 to VR300 if Nvidia does not transition to Kyber at the end of the cycle. In 
our view, Nvidia will do what it can to improve power density, and any current bottlenecks 
should be addressed over the next 12-18 months.

HVDC adoption  
Delta remained the most frequently mentioned name during our meetings. Investor 
attention is centred on the pace of 800V HVDC adoption by Nvidia, especially after 
Semianalysis suggested adoption could be slower than expected in 2027.

Our view is that HVDC is aimed at IT racks with power consumption above 500kW, and 
potentially up to 1.5MW under 800V configurations. Current Vera Rubin racks consume 
about 200kW, or about 400kW, including 100% redundancy, still below the 500kW 
threshold. If die architecture does not further scale and the total chipset count per rack 
remains unchanged, HVDC adoption in Rubin Ultra could remain limited. Based on Delta’s 
current assumptions, adoption in Rubin Ultra could still be above 20-30% in 2028. We 
believe it also echoes our view that we are seeing some adoption of Kyber from 2028.

CPU theme  
Several investors also asked for our view on Aspeed and Lotes, reflecting ongoing interest 
in the CPU server theme over the long term. As mentioned in our previous marketing note, 
CPU servers can serve not only orchestration functions but also inference workloads. This 
view is increasingly well accepted among long-only investors, who appear willing to stay 
invested in CPU beneficiaries through the current component shortage cycle.

Key investment theses  
Price hike > spec upgrade  
From 2023 to 2025, demand for training drove meaningful compute spec upgrades as 
hyperscalers prioritised performance. Looking ahead, we think interest is shifting towards 
inference, which prioritises the cost performance ratio, especially with the emergence of 
agentic AI. As a result, content growth from further spec upgrades should slow versus the 
past two years, even as AI capex continues to expand.

We believe the near-term focus will shift towards price hikes in shortage components, as 
savings from the transition from training to inference are increasingly absorbed by 
constrained supply. In this context, we introduce substrates as a key beneficiary, with the 
most meaningful price hike opportunity likely playing out from 2H26 to 2027. Within 
substrates, NYPCB remains our preferred name. We also like the CCL sector on both price 
hike and spec upgrade trends, although the price hike magnitude should be slower than 
how we look at substrate now. 
We are relatively more 
positive than investors 
on Kyber and HVDC 
adoption in 2028

### Page 5

5 
  Taiwan Data Centre Hardware: 13 July 2026 
Key risks  
Key downside risks for the sector  
Lower-than-expected end-demand from AI companies. We believe the key downside 
risk to our Positive stance on the Taiwan Data Centre Hardware sector is a slowdown in 
the end demand from leading AI companies, such as OpenAI, Anthropic, and xAI. These 
companies’ ability to generate sustainable ARR through monetising foundation models and 
applications ultimately determines the pace of infrastructure investment. If ARR growth 
stalls, hyperscalers may temper their capex plans, leading to weaker-than-expected AI 
server shipments. In our view, this is the primary downside risk to our sector call.

Key risk: lower-than-
expected end-demand 
from AI companies

### Page 1

Summary: Tong Yang released its unaudited June operating results on 14 July 2026 after 
market hours. Its 2Q26 operating profit accounted for 92.2% and 88.1% of our and the 
Bloomberg consensus forecasts, respectively, while its 2Q26 pre-tax profit accounted for 
93.9% and 90.7% of our and the consensus forecasts, respectively. We attribute the miss 
to the weaker-than-expected OEM segment.

We have a Hold (3) rating on Tong Yang with a 12-month TP of TWD81, based on a target 
PER of 14x applied to our 1-year-forward EPS. For more information on the company, 
please refer to our latest flash, Downgrading: waiting for better order visibility, on 1 July.

What’s the impact 
• 2Q26 unaudited results missed due to weaker-than-expected OEM segment. Tong 
Yang’s 2Q26 operating margin was 15.8%, missing our forecast and the Bloomberg 
consensus by 1.4pp and 1.5pp, respectively. As a result, its operating profit of 
TWD892m (+0.6% QoQ and -13.7% YoY) missed our and consensus forecasts by 7.8% 
and 11.9%, respectively. Including an FX loss of TWD20m, its pre-tax profit of 
TWD989m (-16.7% QoQ and +79.3% YoY) also missed our and consensus estimates 
by 6.1% and 9.3%, respectively. We attribute the miss to the weaker-than-expected 
OEM segment.

• Lack of share price catalysts in the near term; however, an attractive dividend 
yield should support the stock. We expect Tong Yang’s revenue momentum to pick

currently trading at PERs of 12.3x/11.7x, vs. its past-3-year trading range of 7-20x. Key 
upside/downside risks: stronger-/weaker-than-expected gross margin expansion and AM 
revenue.

### Page 1

Greater China Technology Hardware
Asia Pacific
Industry View In-Line
2Q26 iPhone build was in-line at 52M units (-7% q/q, +12% y/y), vs. 15-25% q/q
declines usually seen for  2Q. Our checks indicated better-than-seasonal builds this
quarter from major assembly partners including Hon Hai, with iPhone shipments in
June up m/m. This indicates to us that iPhone sell-through continues to trend well
amid memory cost hikes.
We keep our 3Q26 iPhone build estimate unchanged at 54M units (+4% q/q, -2%

y/y). The slower y/y run rate reflects new model SKUs shifting to the premium
segment (18 Pro/Pro Max and iPhone Fold), while the iPhone 18 model is scheduled
for introduction in 1H27. We continue to expect 7-8M iPhone Fold builds in 2H26 (no
change) and will monitor the ramp-up progress to see if there is any upside or
downside to builds.
2Q26 iPad build is in-line with MSe at 13M units (+8% q/q, -10% y/y). The y/y
decline reflects a higher base in 2Q25 given product launches. We keep our 3Q26

iPad build forecast unchanged at 13M units (flat q/q, -7% y/y), as we expect
stocking to remain normal, although memory and material price hikes could have an
impact.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

### Page 1

Macquarie Equity Research
15 July 2026
UMC
Initiation: Trailing edge to take the lead
Key Points
• Sustainable demand recovery, higher utilisation rates, & price hikes for
trailing edge foundry should lead to strong GPM/EPS momentum.
• Other drivers include: fab-lite partnership with Intel at 12nm (and
beyond), TFLN photonics, memory, and advanced packaging.
• We initiate coverage with an Outperform rating and NT$258 TP. We see
upside risk to 2H26-2028 consensus estimates.
Utilisation, ASP, sales growth, margins on the rise: UMC is a
semiconductor foundry focused on mature nodes (through 28/22nm).
We believe we are still in the early stages of an up-cycle and that 2026 is
an inflection point for mature node foundries driven by: 1) an inventory
correction in 2025, 2) increasing demand (e.g., growing power content for
AI-related capex), 3) bifurcation of China and ex-China semiconductor
markets, and 4) major foundries phasing out 8-inch and legacy nodes. UMC
is set to benefit with accelerating revenue, higher utilisation rates, and
higher ASPs, which should all lead to higher margins/EPS growth.
New businesses provide additional upside potential: UMC is embarking on
new leading-edge/new technology partnerships.
• Intel partnership on 12nm: UMC will contribute foundry services and
12nm process technology expertise to provide customers with US-based
manufacturing. We expect this will move beyond 12nm.
• Hyperlight: In 1Q26, UMC signed an agreement for high-volume foundry
production of Hyperlight's TFLN chiplet (both 6" and 8").
• UMC may also partner with memory IP suppliers to mass produce/sell
NAND-like products.
• UMC notes it will also continue to develop advanced packaging, including
3D, with clients.
Upside to consensus forecasts: We believe the combination of higher
utilisation rates, price hikes, and new business will likely spur a round of
consensus upgrades on margins. We initiate with an Outperform rating.
Valuation: Our target price of NT$258 is based on 22x 2028E P/E,
a premium to its 13x historical one-year forward average, given our
expectation of a longer, sustainable up-cycle (higher growth).
Catalysts: A continuation of increasing utilisation rates in 2H26 and 2027,
ASP hikes from 2H26, and high-margin revenue from new businesses.
Investment Thesis and Recommendation
We believe 2026 is a key inflection point for trailing-edge technology at
foundries, which will lead to expanding utilisation rates. Some supply is
being phased out and new capacity is expensive and non-competitive
(benefitting established players). Initiate at Outperform.
Semicons & Semicon Equip
Taiwan
Jeffrey
Ohlweiler
Leo

### Page 2

Macquarie Equity Research UMC
Key Risks to Investment Thesis
• A quick trailing-edge foundry upcycle, where utilisation
rates peak in 2H26 or early 2027.
• Failure to achieve meaningful revenue in any of its joint
venture projects.
• Higher-than-expected costs/inflation that can't be offset
by ASP hikes.
Company Description
United Microelectronics Corporation (UMC) is a
semiconductor foundry that provides wafer manufacturing
services in 8" and 12" Fabs, down to 12nm / FinFET. The firms

has the majority of its production in Taiwan, but also fabs in
China, Japan, Singapore. The firm focuses on mature nodes
and is not engaged in the leading edge EUV manufacturing.
The firm has a large number of specialty processes such as
high voltage, image sensors, automotive.

### Page 3

Macquarie Equity Research UMC
Established trailing-edge
foundries in a solid position
Investment Thesis
We believe 2026 is a key inflection point for trailing-edge technology at foundries which will
lead to at least a few years of utilisation rate growth, before stabilising at a high rate. Both
supply and demand dynamics are driving up utilisation rates (and therefore profitability).
• Demand driven by: 1) an inventory correction in 2025, 2) increasing demand (including

growing power content for AI-related capex), 3) the bifurcation of China and ex-China
semiconductor markets, and 4) content and unit growth for AI edge devices (shift to higher-
end PC/smartphone and emerging growth in robotics and AR).
• Supply driven by: 1) Major players TSMC and Samsung are phasing out 8-inch and trailing-
edge to clear more room for leading-edge and advanced packaging, and 2) new capacity is
expensive and non-competitive (benefitting established players).

Utilisation rates bottomed at high-60% in 2023/24, improved to 75% in 2025, and we believe
are already at mid-80%. We expect utilisation rates to rise to near full over the next quarters.

And, as in prior cycles, as utilisation rates reach high levels (and customers get concerned
about acquiring capacity), ASP hikes should come from 2H26 (we expect a low-single-
digit hike in 2026, and ~10% in 2027). All of this should help drive revenue growth/margin
expansion. We believe this will likely lead to consensus earnings upgrades for 2027/28.

We believe UMC will continue to focus on ROI (consistent with its major strategic shift from

2017), and rely on partnerships to grow into new areas (and more leading-edge nodes).
• Intel partnership on 12nm. UMC will contribute foundry services and 12nm process
technology expertise to provide customers with US-based manufacturing. We expect this
will move beyond 12nm.
• Hyperlight: In 1Q26, UMC signed an agreement for high-volume foundry production of
Hyperlight's TFLN chiplet (both 6" and 8").
• UMC may also partner with memory IP suppliers to mass produce/sell NAND-like products.

We initiate coverage of UMC with an Outperform rating. Our target price of NT$258 is based
on 22x 2028E P/E. We believe the high growth outlook over the next three years justifies our
target multiple premium. Key risks are the semiconductor cycle, and UMC's ability to execute
on its new projects.
Foundry Industry
Trailing-edge foundries should slow down market share bleed. Although we continue to
expect leading-edge foundries' (specifically TSMC) growth to outpace trailing-edge over the

next few years, we believe the steady increase in utilisation rates and ASP hikes at trailing-
edge foundries will slow that widening gap and benefit all key players during 2026-28. UMC's
key advantages for trailing edge: 1) a lot of its capacity is already fully depreciated, 2) it has
good geographic diversity within Asia (Taiwan/China/Japan/Singapore), and 3) it has the
ability to migrate customers beyond 22nm (via Intel partnership).

### Page 6

Macquarie Equity Research UMC
UMC growth driven by ASP,
utilisation rates and new
business... not capacity
expansion
Operations
Up-cycle in trailing-edge foundry: UMC will continue its strategy to focus on ROI and not
aggressive capacity expansion. We expect UMC to rely on partnerships to enter new areas
(and more advanced nodes). Product mix should continue to improve (customers migrate to
22nm), and new ventures should start to contribute materially from 2027. GPM should be

continued to expand through 2028E on: 1) higher utilisation rate, 2) ASP hikes, 3) product
mix, and 4) new project contributions.
New businesses provide additional upside potential: In lieu of aggressive capex, UMC is
entering partnerships to pursue other leading-edge/new technology avenues.
• Partnership with Intel on 12nm (announced in 1Q24): UMC will contribute its foundry
services and 12nm process technology expertise to provide customers with US-based

manufacturing. It enables UMC's existing customers the ability to migrate to 12nm as well
as geographically diversify production to the US. For Intel, it enhances their ability to attract
new foundry customers. We believe this partnership will move beyond 12nm. The company
says it is still on track to deliver PDK and related IP in 2026, with revenue contribution in
2027 (initial application includes DTV, WiFi connectivity and high-speed interface ICs).

• Hyperlight: In 1Q26, UMC signed an agreement with Hyperlight for high-volume foundry
production of their TFLN (Thin-Film Niobate) chiplet (both 6" and 8"). The TFLN optical
products will have extremely high modulation bandwidth with low optical loss, enabling
networking speeds of 1.6T and beyond while significantly reducing power consumption and
laser requirements compared to traditional silicon photonics.
• UMC may also partner with memory IP suppliers to mass produce and sell NAND-like

products to boost utilisation rates faster (UMC has not yet commented on this possibility).
• Advanced packaging: including 3D integration of RF SOI wafers for front-end modules –
UMC notes it will continue to develop with clients.

Expecting GPM expansion
2026-28
Financials
During the early stages of the semiconductor up cycle, UMC's revenues (2024/25) grew low-
to mid-single-digits YoY, as most of the growth was AI-driven and on leading-edge nodes.
However, we believe 2026 is a key inflection point for trailing-edge technology at foundries
which will lead to at least a few years of fast-growing (and then stable higher) utilisation
rates. Both supply and demand side dynamics are driving up utilisation rates (and therefore

profitability).
We forecast revenue to grow 14%/26%25% in 2026E/27E/28E and for GPM to jump
420/770/740bps on higher utilisation rates, higher ASPs, and better product mix. New
businesses should also start to contribute materially from 2027.
With moderate capex, increasing profitability, we expect cash to grow, and its dividend
payout to remain at a 50%+ payout ratio. Non-op gains have also contributed positively –
especially into strong Taiwan tech markets (which continue in 2Q26, but also a risk in case of

a semiconductor down cycle).
1Q26 earnings and 2Q26/2H26 guidance recap
• Revenue +5.5% YoY mainly from shipment increase (shipments up 3% QoQ with utilisation
increasing slightly to 79% from 78%). Blended ASP was down slightly QoQ due to better-
than-expected 8-inch wafer shipment.
• EPS was up ~60% QoQ due to non-op investment gains (strong stock market performance).
• Expect strong wafer shipment growth across 8/12-inch in 2Q (wafer shipments up high-

single digits with utilisation up to low-80%).
• ASP up by low single digits QoQ from product mix improvement (more 22/28nm). GPM
expected ~30%.
• 2H revenue to increase high-double digits HoH (higher utilisation rates and ASP hikes from
2H).

Valuation

Our target price of NT$258 is based on 22x 2028E P/E. Its historical average P/E is 15x
(trailing) and 13x (forward). We use a higher-than-historical-average multiple due to
expectation of a longer, sustainable up-cycle (higher growth), with new projects also likely to
expand margins.
Price to book is also trading above its historical levels. We believe this is reasonable given the
value of its capacity (taking into account a lot of its capacity has already been depreciated,

and the much higher replacement costs for equipment now).
We also expect the current semiconductor cycle could be abnormally long (started
~mid-2023 and we expect it to last until at least 2029), but also starting from 2026, expect
trailing-edge capacity (including 8") to see strong utilisation momentum (2026-27) and the
ability to maintain a high utilisation rate (throughout 2028). We have also seen, during times
of foundry shortages, that foundry companies can maintain over 100% utilisation rates for

several quarters.

### Page 13

Macquarie Equity Research UMC
Key Risks
• We expect the semiconductor cycle (driven by AI capex) to continue at least through 2028.
A earlier peak and down-cycle would negatively impact our forecasts.
• A quick trailing-edge foundry up-cycle, where utilisation rates peak in 2H26 or early 2027
could also impact UMC's ability to maintain high utilisation rates.
• Failure to achieve meaningful revenue in any of its joint venture projects.
• Higher-than-expected costs/inflation that can't be offset by ASP hikes.

• Non-operating income could be at risk in a falling market (has benefitted from the strong
markets over the last several quarters).

Key Quant Findings
The quant model currently holds a marginally positive
view on United Microelectronics Corp.. The strongest
style exposure is Price Momentum, indicating this stock
has had strong medium to long term returns which
often persist into the future. The weakest style exposure
is Profitability, indicating this stock is not efficiently
converting investments to earnings; proxied by ratios like
ROE or ROA.

Price target risks and methodology - UMT 
Valuation: We use a discounted P/E methodology and apply a 37.0x target P/E multiple 
to UMT’s 2029E EPS, discounting it back to 2027E at a COE of 7.8% (beta 1.2x, risk-free 
rate 1.6% and market risk premium at 5.1%), which leads to our 12-month target price 
of NT$2,513. The target P/E is based on the average PEG&M ratio of peers in the 
satellite supply chain. We are Buy-rated on UMT.  
Key Risks: Slower-than-expected LEO satellite deployment; Potential competition from 
new-entrant suppliers; LEO satellite operators manufacturing in-house components.

Source: Company data, Goldman Sachs Research estimates, FactSet. Price as of 16 Jul 2026 close.

### Page 1

M
Idea
2026 full-year revenue guidance a strong beat: TSMC hosted its 2Q26 earnings
call today. It raised its 2026 revenue growth guidance to >40% Y/Y (previously
>30% Y/Y). In our 6/28 preview, we expected guidance to increase to 40% Y/Y vs.
Consensus 35%-40%. Management attribute upside to strong AI demand  despite
challenging consumer demand. CSP customers are rapidly increasing cloud capex.
TSMC did not update its AI semiconductor revenue CAGR forecast but indicated it is

tracking above its previous 55%–60% forecast. We believe a 70%–80% CAGR for
TSMC's AI semiconductor business is reasonable.
Capex hike implies urgent AI demand: TSMC raised its 2026 capex target to US
$60–64bn (vs. the previous US$52–56bn), implying some equipment cost inflation.
We attribute the additional US$8bn to: (1) US$3bn of cost inflation (reflecting a 5%
increase in equipment prices), and (2) US$5bn of equipment prepayments, given
that TSMC is not adding new clean-room capacity this year. These equipment

prepayments suggest urgent AI demand, particularly for agentic AI-related CPUs and
GPUs. Management said A14 would be an even larger node than 2nm and
announced an additional US$100bn of capex for its US fabs, which we believe
supports higher revenue after 2028 than we previously expected.
Do we need to worry about gross margin “miss” and foundry competition? In
response to our question on competition from Samsung Foundry and Intel Foundry,
management indicated that key customers share their five-year production

roadmaps with TSMC, implying TSMC will remain the primary source for customers'
top products. We expect TSMC could increase leading-edge wafer pricing by another
5%–10% in 2027, given the value it provides in leading-edge foundry services. On
gross margin guidance, the 3Q26 midpoint is 66% vs. MSe 67.5%, reflecting dilution
from 2nm. While this falls below some unrealistic 70% Street forecasts, we reiterate
our view to buy on any weakness post-Street margin forecast miss. TSMC

consistently reminds investors about dilution from 2nm and overseas fabs. We do
not believe the margin "miss" reflects foundry competition.
Raise PT to NT$2,988; stay OW: We raise our 2026–2028 revenue assumptions to
reflect stronger AI semiconductor revenue growth. TSMC's high-quality earnings
profile should continue to attract fund flows in a volatile market environment.
Upcoming 2Q26 cloud capex updates from CSPs are likely key catalysts.
What’s Changed

2
2Q26 earnings and 3Q26 guidance review
TSMC hosted  its 2Q26 earnings call on July 16. The company's 2Q26 results and 3Q26
guidance fall in line with our Catalyst preview Scenarios 1 and 2, where we assume the
stock moves up 1%-5% over the coming trading days. See Exhibit 1  and Exhibit 2  for our
review of the quarterly results and guidance.
2Q26 gross margin of 67.7% is roughly in line with MSe 67.4% and above the company's
initial guidance range of 65.5%-67.5%. TSMC's 3Q26 revenue growth guidance of 12% Q/Q

at the mid-point is also in line with our bullish forecast of 10-15% Q/Q. The flattish 3Q26
gross margin figure is in line with our preview, but lower than some bullish buy-side
expectations of 70%. We therefore recommend investors buy on any weakness should the
share price decline following the margin miss.
We now assume 2026-2028 three-year capex of US$213bn vs. US$206bn previously.

TSMC's 2026–2028 foundry floorplan broadly aligns with
our bottom-up demand analysis
Our industry checks suggest ASML allocates capacity based largely on historical demand
and long-term partnerships, indicating that TSMC remains the largest customer for EUV
shipments. If so, TSMC should retain strong pricing power and could increase leading-edge
wafer prices by 5%–10% in 2027, reflecting both the value it delivers to customers and the

potential for margin expansion.

### Page 4

M
Idea
4
TSMC revealed strong capacity growth in 2nm and 3nm with
5nm set to decline
Consistent with our demand forecast, TSMC indicated very strong capacity requirements
for both 2nm and 3nm starting in 2026E, implying a 70% capacity CAGR for N2 from
2026E to 2028E. TSMC also suggested that 5nm capacity will begin to decline from 2027,
in line with our forecast that 5nm demand will fall as Blackwell transitions to Rubin on
3nm.
Separately, advanced packaging capacity expansion should remain a key focus for TSMC in

2027. This includes, but is not limited to, CoWoS and SoIC, for which we now expect
capacity to reach 200kwpm and 70kwpm, respectively, by 2027.

Since nodes below 3nm should also continue to see strong demand, our checks suggest
TSMC's 2nm/1.6nm capacity could approach 100kwpm by end-2026, increase to 150–
170kwpm in 2027, and exceed 200kwpm in 2028. We expect 1.4nm to begin pulling in
tools in 2027, with the bulk of capacity ramping in 2028. Looking further ahead, we expect
TSMC to introduce initial 1.0nm capacity in Tainan in 2029.

Non-AI demand – China's smartphone chip inventory
correction begins, but the impact on TSMC should be limited

In 1Q26, we saw semiconductor industry inventory days increase amid strong AI and
peripheral demand. Some consumer and non-AI customers also refrained from cutting
orders given tight capacity across the supply chain and the prospect of foundry price
increases in 2H26 and 2027. However, we believe it is now time for these non-AI
customers, particularly in smartphones and PCs, to address rising BOM costs either by
reducing orders or passing through higher costs. Even so, we expect the impact on TSMC

to remain limited, as its consumer customer base is concentrated in the premium segment,
where passing through higher costs is generally easier. In addition, high-end PCs and
smartphones primarily consume leading-edge capacity at TSMC, which AI demand can
readily absorb once any wafer capacity becomes available.
Apple provides an example of successful price increases to end customers (see Apple, Inc.:
As Expected, Apple Is Raising Product Pricing (25 Jun 2026) by Erik Woodring) while

maintaining the same wafer demand at TSMC based on our checks.
In contrast, Chinese smartphone shipments are tracking at down 20% Y/Y, compared with
our previous assumption of down 15% Y/Y (see our MediaTek report). Even so, we expect
AI demand to quickly backfill any leading-edge wafer capacity, particularly at 5nm and
below.

Earnings estimate revisions
We raise our earnings forecasts 3% for 2026,  1% for  2027 and 4% for 2028: We raise
our 2026 revenue forecast and modestly increase our margin assumptions to reflect
stronger-than-expected 2Q26 performance, driven by robust AI demand, including
sustained Blackwell shipments. We also raise our 2027–2028 revenue assumptions by
1%–3%, reflecting the increase in 2026 capex guidance from US$54bn to US$62bn at the

midpoint.

Price target  raised to NT$2,988
Our revised price target of NT$2,988 is driven primarily by earnings estimate revisions. It

implies a 20x P/E multiple on our new 2027E EPS forecast. TSMC currently trades at 17x
our 2027E EPS estimate, close to its average NTM P/E of 16.5x since 2018. We find the
stock attractive at current levels.
We continue to derive our 12-month price target (base-case scenario) using a residual
income valuation framework. Our key assumptions remain unchanged, including a cost of
equity of 9.2% (beta of 1.2, equity risk premium of 6.0%, and risk-free rate of 2.0%), an

intermediate growth rate of 10.5%, and a terminal growth rate of 4%.
We also raise our bull-case and bear-case valuations to NT$3,600 (from NT$3,480) and
NT$1,645 (from NT$1,590), respectively.

MS Rating
▪Reﬂecting robust AI capex guidance fromMeta and Microsoft, TSMC remains our TopPick.▪Our estimates reﬂect TSMC’s strongproﬁtability and another year of close to40% Y/Y revenue growth seen for 2026.▪Part of that can be contributed by China'sAI GPU market through Nvidia.▪Reﬂecting increased bargaining power,long-term margin expansion, and sustainableAI semi demand, we expect the stock to re-rate to our implied 2027e target P/E of 20x.

NT$3,600.00
24x 2027e EPS
TSMC dominates foundry services: 1)Breakthrough in EUV tech and materialsaccelerates node migration to 3nm andbeyond. 2) Intel outsources its server CPUproduction to TSMC sooner than expected.3) Intel or Samsung exits leading-edgefoundry business. 4) New tech megatrendssuch as 6G or AI drive global semi revenuegrowth and increase demand for leadingedge nodes. 5) Cost per transistor decreasesfurther along with node migration.

NT$2,988.00
20x 2027e EPS
Leading position in leading-edge logicfoundry services with some gross marginexpansion: 1) Global semi revenue CAGR issustained in the high single digits in 2025-28. 2) TSMC continues to lead in marketshare in 16nm, 7nm, 5nm, 3nm, and 2nm. 3)It continues to observe robust AI-relateddemand. 4) Cost per transistor does notdecrease meaningfully in 3nm and 2nm,while capex per k is more demanding thanfor 7nm and 5nm.
NT$1,645.00

11x 2027e EPS
TSMC's dominance in foundry businesserodes: 1) Global ex-memory semi revenuegrowth is weaker than expected in 2025-28.2) Declining trailing-edge market sharecauses revenue shortfall. 3) Intel'scontribution is weaker than expected. Inteland/or Samsung successfully developleading-edge process technology. 4) Intel'sfoundry business execution provessuccessful and weighs on TSMC with ordercompetition. 5) Demand for 2nm is less thanexpected given high transistor cost forfoundry customers.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

### Page 1

ab 17 July 2026
Global Research
First Read
Wistron
Q2 Preview: Further topline acceleration from
AI servers
Q2 sales ahead on robust AI servers while notebooks lagged
We preview results expected in H1 August where we expect continued strong topline
and outlook driven by upside to AI racks fuelled by Neo Clouds/AI Natives, start of CPU
and Rubin racks from Q4, growth in servers/networking from a low base and work to
qualify new customers for VR200. 2Q sales were above at +6% QoQ to NT$895.4bn,

above prior UBS/Street +4%/+2% with Wistron-only sales (excluding Wiwynn) up 8%
QoQ to NT$617.3bn also above UBS/Street +5%/+1%. We believe rack shipments
ramped from further from 3.9k in Q1 to 4.3k in Q2, offsetting NBs -10% QoQ/-7% YoY
decline to 5.5mn, lower than peers mild growth ahead of further memory inflation. We
maintain our 2Q GM/OpM forecasts in-line with Street at 5.5%/3.5% to lift EPS from NT
$3.59 to NT$3.65, above street NT$3.43.
Raising 2H estimates on stronger servers and start of CPU/VR platforms

Servers are ramping from 30% to 55% of sales on the back of higher NVIDIA racks, new
CPU servers and VR200 from Q426. We estimate Wistron to ramp NVL72 racks from
8.2k to ~10k in H226 and also revise up VR200 from 0.5k to 1k in Q4 as its early
customers ship faster its than peers customers shifting later into Q426, lifting our full
year NVL72 from 16k to 18.5k.  We also expect Wistron's NPI on Vera CPU racks to start
from late Q3 to 2-3k units and NT$60bn  in 2026. Its switch business with a large

US networking OEM is also still on track for 10x growth in 2026.We keep more
conservative on notebooks and model -16% QoQ unit decline in Q326 and -13% YoY
for 2026.
Raising sales growth further with margins upside on new opportunities
We reflect the stronger rack ramp and rising price level for next gen hardware to lift our
2026/27 sales growth estimates from +67%/+44% YoY to +82%/+54% YoY to reach
NT$3.988trn/NT$6.159trn, above Street +60%/+33%. On further mix to lower margin

AMD/VR200 racks, we revise 2026E GM/OpM from 5.6%/3.7% to 5.5%/3.5% while
Wistron-only OpM keeps to its 2.0% target. Our checks indicate Wistron making
progress in customer diversification, with potential 4Q order wins from new customers
and ongoing discussions with Neoclouds on direct business, supporting both revenue
growth and margin expansion.
Maintain Buy and lift our price target  to NT$225 on the upward revision
We lift our 2026E EPS from NT$15.5 to NT$17 reflecting strong 2H outlook and raise

2027E EPS from NT$21.5 to NT$25, above Street at NT$13.9/NT$18.0 respectively. We
lift our PT from NT$215 to NT$225 based on 10x   net debt adjusted '27E EPS (NT$15 net
debt/share) given a weaker balance sheet than peers.

Company Description
Wistron, the manufacturing arm of Acer, became an independent legal entity in 2001.
Following the restructuring, Wistron broadened its customer base to include new customers
such as Dell, HP and Lenovo. Wistron positioned itself as an original design manufacturing

(ODM) company specialising in notebook and desktop PCs, among other IT-related products.
In recent years, it has branched out to include smart devices, servers, GPS devices and LCD TVs
in its product offerings.
UBS EPS and Consensus EPS
The UBS EPS is an adjusted diluted EPS metric. It is calculated using the UBS analyst's
interpretation of earnings suitable for valuation purposes divided by the diluted number of
shares. This may differ to the way the consensus EPS metric has been calculated.

Valuation Method and Risk Statement
We base our price target on PE methodology.
We believe key downside risks for Wistron are: 1) weaker-than-expected PC demand; 2)
larger-than-expected market share losses due to intensified competition; 3) overall demand
for the server business softer than expected; 4) weaker-than-expected production efficiency
impairing margins; and 5) an unfavourable FX rate.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

Global Research
First Read
Hon Hai Precision
Q226 preview: Stronger server growth set to
persist
Q2 Sales beat driven by broad-based strength, AI momentum intact
Hon Hai should report and guide upside on its Q2 earnings release Weds Aug 12th
factoring stronger sales growth (+19% QoQ vs. UBS/Street +11%/+13%) across servers,
compute and components. Computing revenue (+19% YoY) exceeded management's
flattish YoY guidance, supported by strong Macbook demand and pull-ins ahead of

potential price hikes. While the AI server business was relatively flat in June due to
material constraints, Cloud & Networking still grew 27% QoQ and 76% YoY from both
general servers and AI servers (est. AI racks grew from 5.4k in 1Q to 6.7k in Q2).
Smartphone revenue grew 13% YoY within guidance and growing due to the solid
iPhone 17 cycle. Better AI and smartphone pulled through components (+21% YoY) and
above the company's +4–15% YoY guidance range. We model Q2 GM/OpM of

6.1%/3.4% vs. street 3.3% OpM as the higher-margin Cloud & Networking business
supports margins at >50% of total sales vs. smartphones <30%.
Sales should continue expansion led by AI and smartphone launches
We see Q3 sales maintaining strength, lifting our estimates from +4% to +11% QoQ,
above street +6% with growth across smart consumer to start iPhone 18 including Hon
Hai's NPI on the foldable, further ramp of cloud/networking and components still largely

from GB300 with Rubin pushing into Q4. The only lag is computing which could decline
mid-teens from the high H126 base as its customer passes on higher memory costs. We
view margins relatively stable in Q3 with GM/OpM at 6.1%/3.3%.
Lifting 26-27 sales on the better outlook
We lift our 2026-27 sales from +29%/+23% to +34%/+28%, factoring in  1) LPU racks
of 3k/12k in 2026/27 starting from Q426, 2) Vera Rubin ramp-up (model 27.2k/29.0K
NVL72 racks in 26-27 also with ASPs rising), 3) growth of ASIC server as 2nd source into

TPU rack assembly and 4) general server maintaining strong growth on agentic demand
+20-30% YoY.   We model GM/OpM to 6.1%/3.4% and 6.1%/3.3% for 2026-27E,
keeping in-line to better than its 2025 6.1%/3.2% level. The resilient margins are
allowing the strong topline momentum to translate to EPS growth.
Maintain Buy; lift PT from NT$310 to NT$320
We lift 2026-27E EPS from NT$17.3/NT$21.0 to NT$18.00/NT$22.00 and maintain our
Buy with PT lifted from NT$310 to NT$320 at same 15x '27 EPS – the upper half of its

recent range to reflect its leading AI hardware position and opportunity to add value
beyond basic rack assembly (compute/switch board, connectors/power components,
sockets, and new rack introductions). Hon Hai is also at an above-average discount to its
subsidiaries, which account for 73% of its sales and 109% of its profit but also carries
213% of Hon Hai's market cap, a premium vs. historical average 159% of market cap.

Company Description
Hon Hai is the world's largest contract electronics manufacturer (also known by its trade
name, Foxconn). It started connector production in 1981 and has established a strong tooling
fabrication foundation. It diversified into PC enclosures in 1996 and, as a natural extension,

began to offer surface-mount technology assembly/printed circuit board assembly (PCBA)
services in the late 1990s. It moved into electronic manufacturing service PCBA and box-build
in 2000. Its major customers include Apple, Hewlett-Packard, Sony and Dell. Founder and
CEO Terry Gou is the largest shareholder, with a 12.2% stake.
UBS EPS and Consensus EPS
The UBS EPS is an adjusted diluted EPS metric. It is calculated using the UBS analyst's
interpretation of earnings suitable for valuation purposes divided by the diluted number of

shares. This may differ to the way the consensus EPS metric has been calculated.
Valuation Method and Risk Statement
Our price target is based on target PE methodology.
Downside risks for Hon Hai include: 1) slower-than-expected growth and market share loss
for server business; 2) EV projects are delayed or fall short of expectations; 3) a lower-than-
expected market share gain for the iPhone business; 4) weaker-than-expected growth in the
consumer electronic market; and 5) unfavourable FX movement.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

considerations may be lower or higher than portfolios where ESG factors, exclusions, or other sustainability issues are not considered by the portfolio manager, and the
investment opportunities available to such portfolios may differ.

### Page 1

Taiwan Industrials

What’s new: We provide our preview on Airtac’s 2Q26 results.

What’s the impact: Though it has taken longer than expected, we believe 
we are nearing the linear guide (LG) business bearing fruit from 4Q26. After 
a 6-7 year ramp-up, we estimate CNY250m in revenue from LG in 2Q26 
(internal usage: c.CNY75m) and a c.50% utilisation rate. As such, we 
expect LG’s gross margin to gradually expand from the current c.30% to 
c.40% by 2H28E, and believe this will be a key driver of gross margin 
expansion over our forecast horizon. On top of this, we estimate Airtac’s 
electrical controller contributed c.CNY400m (c.5% of total sales) in 2025 
with a c.60% gross margin. We forecast this to reach CNY500m in 2026E. 
Longer term, the company targets c.CNY3bn in annual revenue within the 
next 10 years. For electrical cylinder, we expect this to be introduced by 
2028-29. Given the potential market share gains from the pneumatic 
market (from 30% now to c.35% in the long term) and new growth drivers 
from LG, electrical controllers and electrical cylinders, we believe Airtac 
should deliver teens % revenue CAGR over 2026-30E, alongside improving 
operating margins. While we see a continuing favourable revenue trend at 
Airtac, we see YoY revenue growth decelerating from 4Q26 given the high 
base in 4Q25. As such, we expect share-price momentum to recover on 
revenue growth acceleration in 2Q27E.

2Q26 preview. We now estimate higher 2Q26 sales at TWD12.2bn (up 
21% QoQ and 36% YoY), 10% higher than our previous estimate of 
TWD11.1bn on stronger industrial demand. We estimate the gross margin 
to rise 1.8pp QoQ to 49.8% (vs. our prior estimate of 48.6%) on higher 
leverage on lower fixed costs, and the operating margin to only improve 
1.1pp QoQ to 34.3% (still the highest level over past 10 years) as the China 
government implemented stricter policies on social welfare benefits in 
2Q26. The additional and unrealised cost in 1Q26 was reflected in 2Q26, 
which led to a one-off slightly higher opex ratio. We believe the loss from 
disposal of assets will largely be offset by government subsidies in 2Q26. 
Hence, we estimate net income of TWD3.3bn (EPS: TWD16.63).

What we recommend: We raise our 2026-28E EPS by 10-14% to reflect 
our higher gross-margin assumption. We lift our 12-month TP to TWD1,950 
(from TWD1,840), now based on a target PER of 28x (prior: 31x; the high 
end of its past-3-year range of 18-31x), given the likely earnings growth 
deceleration ahead, on our 1-year forward EPS. We reaffirm our Buy (1) 
call. Downside risk: worse-than-expected pneumatic demand in China.

How we differ: Our 2026-28E EPS are 6-14% higher than the consensus, 
likely due to our more positive view on the company’s operating margin.

Company profile 
Founded in 1988, Airtac is the second-largest manufacturer of pneumatic components in China, with a 
20% market share based on sales volume for 2018. Its key products include cylinder, valves, and filters, 
regulators & lubricators (FRL). The company sells all the products under its own product brand, AirTAC, 
and also provides after-sales services that include installation, maintenance, etc.

quality 2

Capitalmanagement 3
Between2021-23,Airtac'sdividendpayoutratiohasbeenbelow50%.Thepayoutratiowas

研 究  報 告                
昇達科(3491.TT) 
產業：電子–通信網路 
客戶設計變更導致出貨遞延，3Q26 營收重回成⻑軌道  
 投資評價與建議 
看好昇達科受惠低軌衛星部署需求增加，預估2 02 6/ 20 2 7年稅後E P S為
17.81/40.39元，過往產業上行期Forward PE區間落在30~50倍，考量昇達科
為衛星微波與毫米波龍頭供應商，從2027年獲利面評價，給予50倍本益比。  
 重點評論分析 
美系客戶更改設計導致出貨遞延，2Q26營收衰退，3Q26營收重回成⻑軌道：
5/6月營收月減15.1%/13.3%至2.96/2.57億元 ， 係因客戶修改產品設計導致出
貨遞延，惟依照合約客戶需將半成品和成品買回，買回之補償金額將認列於業
外收益，故2Q26營收季減11.6%至9.02億元，預估稅後EPS為3.32元。目前昇
達科持續送樣，產品認證約需6~8週，預計7月下旬公布認證結果，7月底至8
月初訂單開始交貨 ， 預估3Q26營收季增26.4%至11.4億元 ， 稅後EPS為4.42元 。  
低軌衛星即將商業運轉，進入衛星星座高速部署期 ：展望2026~2027年，營
收動能來自：(1)近期SpaceX向美國聯邦通訊委員會(FCC)提交文件申請10萬
顆V3衛星發射，由於V3搭載4頻段(Ka、E、Q/V與W)，頻段加大帶動衛星內部
零組件增加，有利單價提升；(2)Amazon LEO規劃於2029年前部署3,236顆衛
星提供完整服務，AST SpaceMobile衛星提供手機直連服務，預計2026年部
署60~80顆衛星；(3)新客戶Blue Origin預計於4Q27開始部署5,408顆衛星建
立「TeraWave」通訊系統提供企業級與政府機關等高容量與高速資料傳輸。 
 投資風險 
客戶產品出貨時間遞延、發射計畫進度不如預期。

### Page 2

2

| 營運概況  
美系衛星客戶更改設計導致出貨遞延 ， 2Q26營收衰退 ：昇達科5/6月營收分別月減15.1%/13.3%至2.96/2.57億元 ，
係因今年4月下旬開始客戶修改產品設計導致出貨遞延，惟依照訂單合約客戶需將半成品和成品買回，由於無法依
照舊設計出貨將無法認列營收導致5和6月營收衰退 ， 而買回半成品之補償金額將認列於業外收益 ， 故2Q26營收季
減11.6%至9.02億元，預估稅後EPS為3.32元。目前昇達科持續送樣，新產品認證約需6~8週，預計7月下旬客戶
公布認證結果，7月底~8月初訂單開始交貨，預估3Q26營收季增26.4%至11.4億元，稅後EPS為4.42元。 
低軌衛星即將商業運轉，進入衛星星座高速部署期：展望2026至2027年，昇達科營收動能來自：(1)近期SpaceX
向美國聯邦通訊委員會(FCC)提交文件申請10萬顆V3衛星發射，同時SpaceX預計於美國時間2026年7月26日進行
第13次Starship飛行測試且首次搭載20顆V3衛星升空，由於V3搭載4頻段(Ka、E、Q/V與W)，頻段加大帶動衛星
內部零組件增加，有利單價提升；(2)Amazon LEO規劃於2029年前部署3,236顆衛星提供完整服務，AST 
SpaceMobile(ASTS.US)提供手機直連服務，衛星較大顆且內部零組件較多，Content Value較兩大美系客戶高，
ASP與利潤亦較佳，2026年預計將部署60~80顆衛星；(3)新客戶藍色起源(Blue Origin)預計將於4Q27開始部 署
5,408顆衛星建立「TeraWave」通訊系統提供企業級與政府機關等高容量與高速資料傳輸 ； (4)低軌衛星壽命約5~6
年就必須重新發射替換 ， 近期迎來汰換潮 。 綜上所述 ， 預估2026/2027年營收年增85.2%/78.2%至45.4/80.9億元，
毛利率58.0%/64.2%，稅後EPS為17.81/40.39元。
